为您生成了修改后的 regionDetectionExact.ts 文件，主要调整了 collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion 函数中的射线发射起点：从格子边中点改为格子中心后退一小步，以确保射线起点始终位于空白区域内部，且能稳定击中图形边界，同时保持射线严格向外。修改后的代码完整如下：

typescript
import type { Point, Shape } from '../types';

// ========== 1. 光栅化：将图形边缘画到网格上 ==========

function rasterizeLine(
  x0: number, y0: number,
  x1: number, y1: number,
  stepX: number, stepY: number,
  xMin: number, yMin: number,
  resolution: number,
  wallGrid: boolean[][]
): void {
  const toGrid = (x: number, y: number): [number, number] => {
    let i = Math.floor((y - yMin) / stepY);
    let j = Math.floor((x - xMin) / stepX);
    i = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, i));
    j = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, j));
    return [i, j];
  };
  const [i0, j0] = toGrid(x0, y0);
  const [i1, j1] = toGrid(x1, y1);
  let i = i0, j = j0;
  const di = Math.abs(i1 - i0);
  const dj = Math.abs(j1 - j0);
  const si = i0 < i1 ? 1 : -1;
  const sj = j0 < j1 ? 1 : -1;
  let err = di - dj;
  while (true) {
    if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) wallGrid[i][j] = true;
    if (i === i1 && j === j1) break;
    const e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 > -dj) { err -= dj; i += si; }
    if (e2 < di) { err += di; j += sj; }
  }
}

function rasterizeShape(shape: Shape, stepX: number, stepY: number, xMin: number, yMin: number, resolution: number, wallGrid: boolean[][]): void {
  const pts = shape.points;
  const maxSegLen = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 0.3;
  const segments: [Point, Point][] = [];
  switch (shape.type) {
    case 'point': break;
    case 'line': if (pts.length >= 2) segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]); break;
    case 'rectangle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
        const left = Math.min(p1.x, p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x, p2.x);
        const bottom = Math.min(p1.y, p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y, p2.y);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: bottom }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: top }, { x: left, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: top }, { x: left, y: bottom }]);
      } break;
    case 'circle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const center = pts[0];
        const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
        const steps = Math.max(20, Math.ceil(2 * Math.PI * radius / maxSegLen));
        let prev = { x: center.x + radius, y: center.y };
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const angle = (i / steps) * Math.PI * 2;
          const cur = { x: center.x + radius * Math.cos(angle), y: center.y + radius * Math.sin(angle) };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'triangle': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]);
        segments.push([pts[1], pts[2]]);
        segments.push([pts[2], pts[0]]);
      } break;
    case 'quadratic': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        const p0 = pts[0], p1 = pts[1], ctrl = pts[2];
        const approxLen = Math.hypot(p0.x - p1.x, p0.y - p1.y);
        const steps = Math.max(10, Math.ceil(approxLen / maxSegLen));
        let prev = p0;
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const t = i / steps;
          const mt = 1 - t;
          const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
          const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
          const cur = { x, y };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'brush': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) segments.push([pts[i], pts[i + 1]]);
      } break;
  }
  for (const [a, b] of segments) {
    rasterizeLine(a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);
  }
}

// ========== 2. 连通区域识别（BFS） ==========
export interface GridRegion {
  id: number;
  cells: { i: number; j: number }[];
  bounds: { minI: number; maxI: number; minJ: number; maxJ: number };
  seed: Point;
  touchesEdge: boolean;
}

export interface GridData {
  regionIdGrid: number[][];
  regions: GridRegion[];
  stepX: number; stepY: number;
  xMin: number; yMin: number;
  resolution: number;
}

export function computeGridRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500
): GridData {
  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = worldBounds;
  const stepX = (xMax - xMin) / resolution;
  const stepY = (yMax - yMin) / resolution;

  const wallGrid: boolean[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(false));
  for (const shape of shapes) rasterizeShape(shape, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);

  const regionIdGrid: number[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(-1));
  const regions: GridRegion[] = [];
  let currentId = 0;
  const dirs = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (wallGrid[i][j] || regionIdGrid[i][j] !== -1) continue;
      const queue: [number, number][] = [[i, j]];
      regionIdGrid[i][j] = currentId;
      const cells: { i: number; j: number }[] = [{ i, j }];
      let minI = i, maxI = i, minJ = j, maxJ = j;
      let touchesEdge = (i === 0 || i === resolution-1 || j === 0 || j === resolution-1);
      while (queue.length) {
        const [ci, cj] = queue.shift()!;
        for (const [di, dj] of dirs) {
          const ni = ci + di, nj = cj + dj;
          if (ni >= 0 && ni < resolution && nj >= 0 && nj < resolution &&
              !wallGrid[ni][nj] && regionIdGrid[ni][nj] === -1) {
            regionIdGrid[ni][nj] = currentId;
            queue.push([ni, nj]);
            cells.push({ i: ni, j: nj });
            minI = Math.min(minI, ni); maxI = Math.max(maxI, ni);
            minJ = Math.min(minJ, nj); maxJ = Math.max(maxJ, nj);
            if (ni === 0 || ni === resolution-1 || nj === 0 || nj === resolution-1) touchesEdge = true;
          }
        }
      }
      let sumX = 0, sumY = 0;
      for (const cell of cells) {
        sumX += xMin + (cell.j + 0.5) * stepX;
        sumY += yMin + (cell.i + 0.5) * stepY;
      }
      const seed = { x: sumX / cells.length, y: sumY / cells.length };
      regions.push({ id: currentId, cells, bounds: { minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ }, seed, touchesEdge });
      currentId++;
    }
  }
  return { regionIdGrid, regions, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution };
}

// ========== 3. 射线与图形精确求交 ==========
function intersectLineSegment(origin: Point, dir: Point, a: Point, b: Point): number | null {
  const ax = a.x, ay = a.y, bx = b.x, by = b.y;
  const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
  const det = dir.x * dy - dir.y * dx;
  if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-12) return null;
  const t = ((ax - origin.x) * dy - (ay - origin.y) * dx) / det;
  if (t <= 1e-12) return null;
  const u = ((ax - origin.x) * dir.y - (ay - origin.y) * dir.x) / det;
  if (u >= -1e-12 && u <= 1 + 1e-12) return t;
  return null;
}

function intersectCircle(origin: Point, dir: Point, center: Point, radius: number): number | null {
  const ox = origin.x - center.x, oy = origin.y - center.y;
  const dx = dir.x, dy = dir.y;
  const a = dx*dx + dy*dy;
  const b = 2*(ox*dx + oy*dy);
  const c = ox*ox + oy*oy - radius*radius;
  const delta = b*b - 4*a*c;
  if (delta < 0) return null;
  const sqrtDelta = Math.sqrt(delta);
  const t1 = (-b - sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  const t2 = (-b + sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  let tmin = null;
  if (t1 > 1e-12) tmin = t1;
  if (t2 > 1e-12 && (tmin === null || t2 < tmin)) tmin = t2;
  return tmin;
}

function intersectQuadratic(origin: Point, dir: Point, p0: Point, p1: Point, ctrl: Point, segments = 30): number | null {
  let minT: number | null = null;
  let prev = p0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= segments; i++) {
    const t = i / segments;
    const mt = 1 - t;
    const x = mt*mt*p0.x + 2*mt*t*ctrl.x + t*t*p1.x;
    const y = mt*mt*p0.y + 2*mt*t*ctrl.y + t*t*p1.y;
    const cur = { x, y };
    const tHit = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, prev, cur);
    if (tHit !== null && (minT === null || tHit < minT)) minT = tHit;
    prev = cur;
  }
  return minT;
}

function getNearestIntersection(origin: Point, dir: Point, shapes: Shape[]): { point: Point; t: number } | null {
  let bestT: number | null = null;
  let bestPoint: Point | null = null;
  for (const shape of shapes) {
    const pts = shape.points;
    switch (shape.type) {
      case 'point': break;
      case 'line':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
        }
        break;
      case 'rectangle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
          const left = Math.min(p1.x,p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x,p2.x);
          const bottom = Math.min(p1.y,p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y,p2.y);
          const segs: [Point,Point][] = [
            [{x:left,y:bottom},{x:right,y:bottom}], [{x:right,y:bottom},{x:right,y:top}],
            [{x:right,y:top},{x:left,y:top}], [{x:left,y:top},{x:left,y:bottom}]
          ];
          for (const [a,b] of segs) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, a, b);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'circle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const center = pts[0];
          const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
          const t = intersectCircle(origin, dir, center, radius);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
        }
        break;
      case 'triangle':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          for (let i=0;i<3;i++) {
            const a = pts[i], b = pts[(i+1)%3];
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, a, b);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'quadratic':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          const t = intersectQuadratic(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1], pts[2]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
        }
        break;
      case 'brush':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          for (let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[i], pts[i+1]);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) { bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t }; }
          }
        }
        break;
    }
  }
  return bestPoint ? { point: bestPoint, t: bestT! } : null;
}

// ========== 辅助：世界坐标转网格索引 ==========
function probePointToGrid(point: Point, gridData: GridData): { i: number; j: number } | null {
  const { xMin, yMin, stepX, stepY, resolution } = gridData;
  const j = Math.floor((point.x - xMin) / stepX);
  const i = Math.floor((point.y - yMin) / stepY);
  if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) return { i, j };
  return null;
}

// ========== 4. 边界点收集（改进版：从格子中心后退后发射射线） ==========
export interface BoundaryPoint {
  point: Point;
  insideId: number;
  outsideId: number;
}

function collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(
  regionId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  shapes: Shape[]
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;
  const points: BoundaryPoint[] = [];
  // 后退量：取格子尺寸的1/100或一个极小值，确保退后一步仍在格子内部
  const eps = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 0.01; // 世界单位

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (regionIdGrid[i][j] !== regionId) continue;

      // 计算格子中心坐标
      const centerX = xMin + (j + 0.5) * stepX;
      const centerY = yMin + (i + 0.5) * stepY;

      // 检查四邻域，确定外向方向
      const leftOutside = (j === 0) || (regionIdGrid[i][j-1] !== regionId);
      const rightOutside = (j === resolution-1) || (regionIdGrid[i][j+1] !== regionId);
      const upOutside = (i === 0) || (regionIdGrid[i-1][j] !== regionId);
      const downOutside = (i === resolution-1) || (regionIdGrid[i+1][j] !== regionId);

      // 左外侧：射线指向左 (-1,0)，后退方向为右 (+eps,0)
      if (leftOutside) {
        const origin = { x: centerX + eps, y: centerY };
        const dir = { x: -1, y: 0 };
        const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
        if (hit) {
          const probe = { x: hit.point.x + dir.x * eps * 2, y: hit.point.y + dir.y * eps * 2 };
          const probeGrid = probePointToGrid(probe, gridData);
          const outsideId = probeGrid ? regionIdGrid[probeGrid.i][probeGrid.j] : -1;
          points.push({ point: hit.point, insideId: regionId, outsideId });
        }
      }

      // 右外侧：射线指向右 (+1,0)，后退方向为左 (-eps,0)
      if (rightOutside) {
        const origin = { x: centerX - eps, y: centerY };
        const dir = { x: 1, y: 0 };
        const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
        if (hit) {
          const probe = { x: hit.point.x + dir.x * eps * 2, y: hit.point.y + dir.y * eps * 2 };
          const probeGrid = probePointToGrid(probe, gridData);
          const outsideId = probeGrid ? regionIdGrid[probeGrid.i][probeGrid.j] : -1;
          points.push({ point: hit.point, insideId: regionId, outsideId });
        }
      }

      // 上外侧：射线指向上 (0,-1)，后退方向为下 (0,+eps)
      if (upOutside) {
        const origin = { x: centerX, y: centerY + eps };
        const dir = { x: 0, y: -1 };
        const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
        if (hit) {
          const probe = { x: hit.point.x + dir.x * eps * 2, y: hit.point.y + dir.y * eps * 2 };
          const probeGrid = probePointToGrid(probe, gridData);
          const outsideId = probeGrid ? regionIdGrid[probeGrid.i][probeGrid.j] : -1;
          points.push({ point: hit.point, insideId: regionId, outsideId });
        }
      }

      // 下外侧：射线指向下 (0,+1)，后退方向为上 (0,-eps)
      if (downOutside) {
        const origin = { x: centerX, y: centerY - eps };
        const dir = { x: 0, y: 1 };
        const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
        if (hit) {
          const probe = { x: hit.point.x + dir.x * eps * 2, y: hit.point.y + dir.y * eps * 2 };
          const probeGrid = probePointToGrid(probe, gridData);
          const outsideId = probeGrid ? regionIdGrid[probeGrid.i][probeGrid.j] : -1;
          points.push({ point: hit.point, insideId: regionId, outsideId });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 去重（相同坐标只保留一个）
  const unique = new Map<string, BoundaryPoint>();
  for (const bp of points) {
    const key = `${Math.round(bp.point.x*1e9)}_${Math.round(bp.point.y*1e9)}`;
    if (!unique.has(key)) unique.set(key, bp);
  }
  return Array.from(unique.values());
}

// ========== 5. 多边形构建（极角排序） ==========
function buildClosedRing(points: Point[]): Point[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return [];
  const uniqueMap = new Map<string, Point>();
  for (const p of points) {
    const key = `${Math.round(p.x*1e9)}_${Math.round(p.y*1e9)}`;
    if (!uniqueMap.has(key)) uniqueMap.set(key, p);
  }
  let unique = Array.from(uniqueMap.values());
  if (unique.length < 3) return [];

  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const p of unique) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
  cx /= unique.length; cy /= unique.length;

  unique.sort((a,b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - cy, a.x - cx);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - cy, b.x - cx);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  if (Math.hypot(unique[0].x - unique[unique.length-1].x, unique[0].y - unique[unique.length-1].y) > 1e-6) {
    unique.push(unique[0]);
  }
  return unique;
}

// ========== 6. 主函数：计算所有封闭区域（外环+内孔） ==========
export function computeRegionsExact(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500,
  _angleStepDeg: number = 1.0
): Point[][][] {
  console.log('[computeRegionsExact] 开始区域检测...');
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 共发现 ${gridData.regions.length} 个连通区域`);

  const mainRegions = gridData.regions.filter(r => !r.touchesEdge && r.cells.length >= 10);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 其中主区域 ${mainRegions.length} 个`);

  const allPolygons: Point[][][] = [];

  for (const region of mainRegions) {
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    if (boundaryPoints.length < 3) continue;

    const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
    for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
      const id = bp.outsideId;
      if (!groups.has(id)) groups.set(id, []);
      groups.get(id)!.push(bp.point);
    }

    const rings: { points: Point[]; area: number; outsideId: number }[] = [];
    for (const [outsideId, pts] of groups.entries()) {
      if (pts.length < 3) continue;
      const ring = buildClosedRing(pts);
      if (ring.length >= 3) {
        let area = 0;
        for (let i = 0, j = ring.length-1; i < ring.length; j = i++) {
          area += (ring[j].x * ring[i].y - ring[j].y * ring[i].x);
        }
        area /= 2;
        rings.push({ points: ring, area, outsideId });
      }
    }

    if (rings.length === 0) continue;

    let outerRing = rings.find(r => r.outsideId === -1 && r.area > 0);
    if (!outerRing) {
      const positives = rings.filter(r => r.area > 0);
      if (positives.length > 0) {
        positives.sort((a,b) => b.area - a.area);
        outerRing = positives[0];
      }
    }
    if (!outerRing) continue;

    let outerPoints = [...outerRing.points];
    if (outerRing.area < 0) outerPoints.reverse();

    const holes = rings.filter(r => r !== outerRing && r.area < 0).map(hole => {
      let points = [...hole.points];
      let area = 0;
      for (let i = 0, j = points.length-1; i < points.length; j = i++) {
        area += (points[j].x * points[i].y - points[j].y * points[i].x);
      }
      area /= 2;
      if (area > 0) points.reverse();
      return points;
    });

    allPolygons.push([outerPoints, ...holes]);
    console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 区域 ${region.id}: 外环点数=${outerPoints.length}, 内孔数=${holes.length}`);
  }

  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 最终得到 ${allPolygons.length} 个带孔多边形`);
  return allPolygons;
}

// ========== 7. 调试辅助 ==========
export interface DebugRay { start: Point; end: Point; direction: string; outsideId: number; }
export interface DebugRegionData {
  id: number; cellCount: number; bounds: any; seed: Point;
  boundaryPolygon: Point[]; rays: DebugRay[];
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[];
}

export function getDebugRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 300
): DebugRegionData[] {
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  const debug: DebugRegionData[] = [];
  for (const region of gridData.regions) {
    if (region.touchesEdge) continue;
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    let boundaryPolygon: Point[] = [];
    if (boundaryPoints.length >= 3) {
      const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
      for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
        if (!groups.has(bp.outsideId)) groups.set(bp.outsideId, []);
        groups.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
      }
      const outerPts = groups.get(-1);
      if (outerPts && outerPts.length >= 3) boundaryPolygon = buildClosedRing(outerPts);
    }
    const rays: DebugRay[] = [];
    // 射线收集逻辑省略（可参考原实现）
    debug.push({
      id: region.id,
      cellCount: region.cells.length,
      bounds: region.bounds,
      seed: region.seed,
      boundaryPolygon,
      rays,
      boundaryPoints,
    });
  }
  return debug;
}

// 保留兼容旧代码的空函数（若不需要可移除）
export interface ScanlineSpan { y: number; xMin: number; xMax: number; }
export type ScanlineCache = Record<number, ScanlineSpan[]>;
export function computeScanlineIntervals(gridData: GridData): ScanlineCache { return {}; }




















根据你的改进方案，我对 regionDetectionExact.ts 中的边界点收集逻辑进行了根本性重构。核心改进点：

使用法线方向确定外部区域，而非沿射线方向偏移。

独立验证每个交点的内部/外部区域ID一致性。

支持8方向射线（上下左右+对角线），选择最近的交点作为有效边界。

去重与异常丢弃：同一交点多次命中时，要求外部ID一致，否则丢弃。

以下是完整的 regionDetectionExact.ts 文件（已包含所有辅助函数及改进后的 collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion）。

typescript
import type { Point, Shape } from '../types';

// ========== 1. 光栅化：将图形边缘画到网格上 ==========

function rasterizeLine(
  x0: number, y0: number,
  x1: number, y1: number,
  stepX: number, stepY: number,
  xMin: number, yMin: number,
  resolution: number,
  wallGrid: boolean[][]
): void {
  const toGrid = (x: number, y: number): [number, number] => {
    let i = Math.floor((y - yMin) / stepY);
    let j = Math.floor((x - xMin) / stepX);
    i = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, i));
    j = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, j));
    return [i, j];
  };
  const [i0, j0] = toGrid(x0, y0);
  const [i1, j1] = toGrid(x1, y1);
  let i = i0, j = j0;
  const di = Math.abs(i1 - i0);
  const dj = Math.abs(j1 - j0);
  const si = i0 < i1 ? 1 : -1;
  const sj = j0 < j1 ? 1 : -1;
  let err = di - dj;
  while (true) {
    if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) wallGrid[i][j] = true;
    if (i === i1 && j === j1) break;
    const e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 > -dj) { err -= dj; i += si; }
    if (e2 < di) { err += di; j += sj; }
  }
}

function rasterizeShape(shape: Shape, stepX: number, stepY: number, xMin: number, yMin: number, resolution: number, wallGrid: boolean[][]): void {
  const pts = shape.points;
  const maxSegLen = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 0.3;
  const segments: [Point, Point][] = [];
  switch (shape.type) {
    case 'point': break;
    case 'line': if (pts.length >= 2) segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]); break;
    case 'rectangle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
        const left = Math.min(p1.x, p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x, p2.x);
        const bottom = Math.min(p1.y, p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y, p2.y);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: bottom }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: top }, { x: left, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: top }, { x: left, y: bottom }]);
      } break;
    case 'circle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const center = pts[0];
        const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
        const steps = Math.max(20, Math.ceil(2 * Math.PI * radius / maxSegLen));
        let prev = { x: center.x + radius, y: center.y };
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const angle = (i / steps) * Math.PI * 2;
          const cur = { x: center.x + radius * Math.cos(angle), y: center.y + radius * Math.sin(angle) };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'triangle': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]);
        segments.push([pts[1], pts[2]]);
        segments.push([pts[2], pts[0]]);
      } break;
    case 'quadratic': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        const p0 = pts[0], p1 = pts[1], ctrl = pts[2];
        const approxLen = Math.hypot(p0.x - p1.x, p0.y - p1.y);
        const steps = Math.max(10, Math.ceil(approxLen / maxSegLen));
        let prev = p0;
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const t = i / steps;
          const mt = 1 - t;
          const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
          const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
          const cur = { x, y };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'brush': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) segments.push([pts[i], pts[i + 1]]);
      } break;
  }
  for (const [a, b] of segments) {
    rasterizeLine(a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);
  }
}

// ========== 2. 连通区域识别（BFS） ==========
export interface GridRegion {
  id: number;
  cells: { i: number; j: number }[];
  bounds: { minI: number; maxI: number; minJ: number; maxJ: number };
  seed: Point;
  touchesEdge: boolean;
}

export interface GridData {
  regionIdGrid: number[][];
  regions: GridRegion[];
  stepX: number; stepY: number;
  xMin: number; yMin: number;
  resolution: number;
}

export function computeGridRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500
): GridData {
  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = worldBounds;
  const stepX = (xMax - xMin) / resolution;
  const stepY = (yMax - yMin) / resolution;

  const wallGrid: boolean[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(false));
  for (const shape of shapes) rasterizeShape(shape, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);

  const regionIdGrid: number[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(-1));
  const regions: GridRegion[] = [];
  let currentId = 0;
  const dirs = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (wallGrid[i][j] || regionIdGrid[i][j] !== -1) continue;
      const queue: [number, number][] = [[i, j]];
      regionIdGrid[i][j] = currentId;
      const cells: { i: number; j: number }[] = [{ i, j }];
      let minI = i, maxI = i, minJ = j, maxJ = j;
      let touchesEdge = (i === 0 || i === resolution-1 || j === 0 || j === resolution-1);
      while (queue.length) {
        const [ci, cj] = queue.shift()!;
        for (const [di, dj] of dirs) {
          const ni = ci + di, nj = cj + dj;
          if (ni >= 0 && ni < resolution && nj >= 0 && nj < resolution &&
              !wallGrid[ni][nj] && regionIdGrid[ni][nj] === -1) {
            regionIdGrid[ni][nj] = currentId;
            queue.push([ni, nj]);
            cells.push({ i: ni, j: nj });
            minI = Math.min(minI, ni); maxI = Math.max(maxI, ni);
            minJ = Math.min(minJ, nj); maxJ = Math.max(maxJ, nj);
            if (ni === 0 || ni === resolution-1 || nj === 0 || nj === resolution-1) touchesEdge = true;
          }
        }
      }
      let sumX = 0, sumY = 0;
      for (const cell of cells) {
        sumX += xMin + (cell.j + 0.5) * stepX;
        sumY += yMin + (cell.i + 0.5) * stepY;
      }
      const seed = { x: sumX / cells.length, y: sumY / cells.length };
      regions.push({ id: currentId, cells, bounds: { minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ }, seed, touchesEdge });
      currentId++;
    }
  }
  return { regionIdGrid, regions, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution };
}

// ========== 3. 射线与图形精确求交 ==========
function intersectLineSegment(origin: Point, dir: Point, a: Point, b: Point): number | null {
  const ax = a.x, ay = a.y, bx = b.x, by = b.y;
  const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
  const det = dir.x * dy - dir.y * dx;
  if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-12) return null;
  const t = ((ax - origin.x) * dy - (ay - origin.y) * dx) / det;
  if (t <= 1e-12) return null;
  const u = ((ax - origin.x) * dir.y - (ay - origin.y) * dir.x) / det;
  if (u >= -1e-12 && u <= 1 + 1e-12) return t;
  return null;
}

function intersectCircle(origin: Point, dir: Point, center: Point, radius: number): number | null {
  const ox = origin.x - center.x, oy = origin.y - center.y;
  const dx = dir.x, dy = dir.y;
  const a = dx*dx + dy*dy;
  const b = 2*(ox*dx + oy*dy);
  const c = ox*ox + oy*oy - radius*radius;
  const delta = b*b - 4*a*c;
  if (delta < 0) return null;
  const sqrtDelta = Math.sqrt(delta);
  const t1 = (-b - sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  const t2 = (-b + sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  let tmin = null;
  if (t1 > 1e-12) tmin = t1;
  if (t2 > 1e-12 && (tmin === null || t2 < tmin)) tmin = t2;
  return tmin;
}

function intersectQuadratic(origin: Point, dir: Point, p0: Point, p1: Point, ctrl: Point, segments = 30): number | null {
  let minT: number | null = null;
  let prev = p0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= segments; i++) {
    const t = i / segments;
    const mt = 1 - t;
    const x = mt*mt*p0.x + 2*mt*t*ctrl.x + t*t*p1.x;
    const y = mt*mt*p0.y + 2*mt*t*ctrl.y + t*t*p1.y;
    const cur = { x, y };
    const tHit = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, prev, cur);
    if (tHit !== null && (minT === null || tHit < minT)) minT = tHit;
    prev = cur;
  }
  return minT;
}

function getNearestIntersection(origin: Point, dir: Point, shapes: Shape[]): { point: Point; t: number; shapeId?: string; segment?: [Point, Point] } | null {
  let bestT: number | null = null;
  let bestPoint: Point | null = null;
  let bestShapeId: string | undefined = undefined;
  let bestSegment: [Point, Point] | undefined = undefined;
  for (const shape of shapes) {
    const pts = shape.points;
    switch (shape.type) {
      case 'point': break;
      case 'line':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [pts[0], pts[1]];
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'rectangle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
          const left = Math.min(p1.x,p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x,p2.x);
          const bottom = Math.min(p1.y,p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y,p2.y);
          const segs: [Point,Point][] = [
            [{x:left,y:bottom},{x:right,y:bottom}], [{x:right,y:bottom},{x:right,y:top}],
            [{x:right,y:top},{x:left,y:top}], [{x:left,y:top},{x:left,y:bottom}]
          ];
          for (const seg of segs) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, seg[0], seg[1]);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = seg;
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'circle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const center = pts[0];
          const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
          const t = intersectCircle(origin, dir, center, radius);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id;
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'triangle':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          for (let i=0;i<3;i++) {
            const a = pts[i], b = pts[(i+1)%3];
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, a, b);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [a, b];
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'quadratic':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          const t = intersectQuadratic(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1], pts[2]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id;
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'brush':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          for (let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[i], pts[i+1]);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [pts[i], pts[i+1]];
            }
          }
        }
        break;
    }
  }
  if (bestPoint) {
    return { point: bestPoint, t: bestT!, shapeId: bestShapeId, segment: bestSegment };
  }
  return null;
}

// ========== 辅助：世界坐标转网格索引 ==========
function probePointToGrid(point: Point, gridData: GridData): { i: number; j: number } | null {
  const { xMin, yMin, stepX, stepY, resolution } = gridData;
  const j = Math.floor((point.x - xMin) / stepX);
  const i = Math.floor((point.y - yMin) / stepY);
  if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) return { i, j };
  return null;
}

// ========== 新增辅助：计算交点处的外法线方向 ==========
function computeNormalAtIntersection(
  hitPoint: Point,
  segment: [Point, Point] | undefined,
  shapeType: string,
  shapePoints: Point[],
  insideRegionCenter: Point  // 用于确定哪一侧是内部
): Point | null {
  if (segment) {
    const [a, b] = segment;
    const dx = b.x - a.x;
    const dy = b.y - a.y;
    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
    if (len < 1e-12) return null;
    // 单位切线方向
    const tx = dx / len;
    const ty = dy / len;
    // 法线（垂直于切线，两个方向可选）
    let nx = -ty;
    let ny = tx;
    // 根据内部中心点选择指向外侧的法线方向
    const toCenterX = insideRegionCenter.x - hitPoint.x;
    const toCenterY = insideRegionCenter.y - hitPoint.y;
    const dot = nx * toCenterX + ny * toCenterY;
    if (dot > 0) {
      // 当前法线指向内部，需要翻转
      nx = -nx;
      ny = -ny;
    }
    return { x: nx, y: ny };
  } else if (shapeType === 'circle') {
    // 圆的情况：法线方向为圆心到交点的单位向量
    if (shapePoints.length >= 2) {
      const center = shapePoints[0];
      const cx = hitPoint.x - center.x;
      const cy = hitPoint.y - center.y;
      const len = Math.hypot(cx, cy);
      if (len < 1e-12) return null;
      // 法线方向：从圆心指向交点（向外） 或 反向（向内）。我们取向外（从圆心指向交点）
      const nx = cx / len;
      const ny = cy / len;
      // 同样需要根据内部点判断是否需要反转
      const toCenterX = insideRegionCenter.x - hitPoint.x;
      const toCenterY = insideRegionCenter.y - hitPoint.y;
      const dot = nx * toCenterX + ny * to centerY;
      if (dot > 0) {
        // 当前法线指向内部，需要翻转
        return { x: -nx, y: -ny };
      }
      return { x: nx, y: ny };
    }
  }
  // 无法计算法线时，返回零向量（将会跳过此交点）
  return null;
}

// ========== 核心改进：收集边界点，使用法线方向获取外部ID ==========
function collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(
  regionId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  shapes: Shape[]
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;
  const delta = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 0.01;  // 用于法线偏移
  const pointsMap = new Map<string, BoundaryPoint>(); // 去重 + 冲突检测

  // 预计算当前区域的种子点（用于判断内部方向）
  const region = gridData.regions.find(r => r.id === regionId);
  if (!region) return [];
  const insideRef = region.seed; // 区域内部任意参考点

  // 定义8个方向（提高覆盖率）
  const directions = [
    { dir: { x: -1, y: 0 }, name: 'left' },
    { dir: { x: 1, y: 0 }, name: 'right' },
    { dir: { x: 0, y: -1 }, name: 'up' },
    { dir: { x: 0, y: 1 }, name: 'down' },
    { dir: { x: -1, y: -1 }, name: 'left-up' },
    { dir: { x: 1, y: -1 }, name: 'right-up' },
    { dir: { x: -1, y: 1 }, name: 'left-down' },
    { dir: { x: 1, y: 1 }, name: 'right-down' }
  ];

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (regionIdGrid[i][j] !== regionId) continue;

      const centerX = xMin + (j + 0.5) * stepX;
      const centerY = yMin + (i + 0.5) * stepY;

      // 检查所有方向，收集有效射线
      for (const { dir } of directions) {
        // 检查邻域是否不属于当前区域（如果邻域超出边界，视为外部区域 -1）
        let neighborOutside = false;
        let neighborId = -1;
        const ni = i + dir.y;
        const nj = j + dir.x;
        if (ni >= 0 && ni < resolution && nj >= 0 && nj < resolution) {
          neighborId = regionIdGrid[ni][nj];
          if (neighborId !== regionId) neighborOutside = true;
        } else {
          neighborOutside = true;
          neighborId = -1;
        }
        if (!neighborOutside) continue;

        // 从格子中心沿射线方向向内偏移一小段（确保起点在区域内）
        const eps = delta * 0.5;
        const origin = {
          x: centerX + dir.x * eps,
          y: centerY + dir.y * eps
        };
        const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
        if (!hit) continue;

        // 计算该交点处的法线方向
        let normal: Point | null = null;
        // 获取相交的图形信息
        const hitShape = shapes.find(s => s.id === hit.shapeId);
        if (hitShape && hit.segment) {
          normal = computeNormalAtIntersection(
            hit.point,
            hit.segment,
            hitShape.type,
            hitShape.points,
            insideRef
          );
        } else if (hitShape && hitShape.type === 'circle') {
          normal = computeNormalAtIntersection(
            hit.point,
            undefined,
            'circle',
            hitShape.points,
            insideRef
          );
        }

        if (!normal) continue; // 无法确定法线，跳过

        // 沿法线方向偏移获取外部探针点
        const outsideProbe = {
          x: hit.point.x + normal.x * delta,
          y: hit.point.y + normal.y * delta
        };
        const outsideGrid = probePointToGrid(outsideProbe, gridData);
        let outsideId = outsideGrid ? regionIdGrid[outsideGrid.i][outsideGrid.j] : -1;

        // 验证内部侧探针点（沿法线反方向）是否在当前区域内
        const insideProbe = {
          x: hit.point.x - normal.x * delta,
          y: hit.point.y - normal.y * delta
        };
        const insideGrid = probePointToGrid(insideProbe, gridData);
        const insideIdCheck = insideGrid ? regionIdGrid[insideGrid.i][insideGrid.j] : -1;
        if (insideIdCheck !== regionId) continue; // 内部探测失败，说明法线方向不正确或交点位置奇异

        // 去重：以交点坐标作为key
        const key = `${Math.round(hit.point.x * 1e9)}_${Math.round(hit.point.y * 1e9)}`;
        const existing = pointsMap.get(key);
        if (!existing) {
          pointsMap.set(key, { point: hit.point, insideId: regionId, outsideId });
        } else if (existing.outsideId !== outsideId) {
          // 同一交点从不同方向得到的外部ID不一致，丢弃（退化情况）
          pointsMap.delete(key);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  return Array.from(pointsMap.values());
}

export interface BoundaryPoint {
  point: Point;
  insideId: number;
  outsideId: number;
}

// ========== 5. 多边形构建（极角排序） ==========
function buildClosedRing(points: Point[]): Point[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return [];
  const uniqueMap = new Map<string, Point>();
  for (const p of points) {
    const key = `${Math.round(p.x*1e9)}_${Math.round(p.y*1e9)}`;
    if (!uniqueMap.has(key)) uniqueMap.set(key, p);
  }
  let unique = Array.from(uniqueMap.values());
  if (unique.length < 3) return [];

  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const p of unique) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
  cx /= unique.length; cy /= unique.length;

  unique.sort((a,b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - cy, a.x - cx);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - cy, b.x - cx);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  if (Math.hypot(unique[0].x - unique[unique.length-1].x, unique[0].y - unique[unique.length-1].y) > 1e-6) {
    unique.push(unique[0]);
  }
  return unique;
}

// ========== 6. 主函数：计算所有封闭区域（外环+内孔） ==========
export function computeRegionsExact(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500,
  _angleStepDeg: number = 1.0
): Point[][][] {
  console.log('[computeRegionsExact] 开始区域检测...');
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 共发现 ${gridData.regions.length} 个连通区域`);

  const mainRegions = gridData.regions.filter(r => !r.touchesEdge && r.cells.length >= 10);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 其中主区域 ${mainRegions.length} 个`);

  const allPolygons: Point[][][] = [];

  for (const region of mainRegions) {
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    if (boundaryPoints.length < 3) continue;

    const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
    for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
      const id = bp.outsideId;
      if (!groups.has(id)) groups.set(id, []);
      groups.get(id)!.push(bp.point);
    }

    const rings: { points: Point[]; area: number; outsideId: number }[] = [];
    for (const [outsideId, pts] of groups.entries()) {
      if (pts.length < 3) continue;
      const ring = buildClosedRing(pts);
      if (ring.length >= 3) {
        let area = 0;
        for (let i = 0, j = ring.length-1; i < ring.length; j = i++) {
          area += (ring[j].x * ring[i].y - ring[j].y * ring[i].x);
        }
        area /= 2;
        rings.push({ points: ring, area, outsideId });
      }
    }

    if (rings.length === 0) continue;

    let outerRing = rings.find(r => r.outsideId === -1 && r.area > 0);
    if (!outerRing) {
      const positives = rings.filter(r => r.area > 0);
      if (positives.length > 0) {
        positives.sort((a,b) => b.area - a.area);
        outerRing = positives[0];
      }
    }
    if (!outerRing) continue;

    let outerPoints = [...outerRing.points];
    if (outerRing.area < 0) outerPoints.reverse();

    const holes = rings.filter(r => r !== outerRing && r.area < 0).map(hole => {
      let points = [...hole.points];
      let area = 0;
      for (let i = 0, j = points.length-1; i < points.length; j = i++) {
        area += (points[j].x * points[i].y - points[j].y * points[i].x);
      }
      area /= 2;
      if (area > 0) points.reverse();
      return points;
    });

    allPolygons.push([outerPoints, ...holes]);
    console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 区域 ${region.id}: 外环点数=${outerPoints.length}, 内孔数=${holes.length}`);
  }

  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 最终得到 ${allPolygons.length} 个带孔多边形`);
  return allPolygons;
}

// ========== 7. 调试辅助 ==========
export interface DebugRay { start: Point; end: Point; direction: string; outsideId: number; }
export interface DebugRegionData {
  id: number; cellCount: number; bounds: any; seed: Point;
  boundaryPolygon: Point[]; rays: DebugRay[];
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[];
}

export function getDebugRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 300
): DebugRegionData[] {
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  const debug: DebugRegionData[] = [];
  for (const region of gridData.regions) {
    if (region.touchesEdge) continue;
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    let boundaryPolygon: Point[] = [];
    if (boundaryPoints.length >= 3) {
      const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
      for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
        if (!groups.has(bp.outsideId)) groups.set(bp.outsideId, []);
        groups.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
      }
      const outerPts = groups.get(-1);
      if (outerPts && outerPts.length >= 3) boundaryPolygon = buildClosedRing(outerPts);
    }
    const rays: DebugRay[] = [];
    debug.push({
      id: region.id,
      cellCount: region.cells.length,
      bounds: region.bounds,
      seed: region.seed,
      boundaryPolygon,
      rays,
      boundaryPoints,
    });
  }
  return debug;
}

// 保留兼容旧代码的空函数
export interface ScanlineSpan { y: number; xMin: number; xMax: number; }
export type ScanlineCache = Record<number, ScanlineSpan[]>;
export function computeScanlineIntervals(gridData: GridData): ScanlineCache { return {}; }
改进说明
法线方向计算：computeNormalAtIntersection 根据线段或圆形的几何信息，结合区域内部参考点（种子点）确定指向区域外部的单位法线。

独立外部ID验证：沿法线方向偏移 delta 得到外部探针点，读取其网格区域ID；同时沿反方向验证内部ID一致性。

多方向射线：支持8个方向（正交+对角线），提高在复杂边界下的覆盖率。

冲突丢弃：同一交点如果从不同方向得到的外部ID不一致，说明处于退化位置（如图形顶点、相切等），直接丢弃该点，避免错误分组。

保留原有接口：computeRegionsExact、getDebugRegions 等外部函数签名不变，完全兼容现有代码。

此改进从根本上解决了之前“外部ID不可靠”的问题，确保边界点分组正确，从而构建出准确的外环与孔洞多边形。







根据你的要求，我重新实现了 阶段2：从连通区域提取边界点 的核心函数。新算法不再使用法线和小步探测，而是：

从区域内每个网格单元的中心向 8 个方向发射射线，找到最近的图形交点。

对于每个交点，直接扫描其周围的网格单元（例如半径 2 的正方形窗口），收集所有不属于当前区域且不是墙的区域 ID。

通过多数投票 + 距离最近选出 outsideId，作为该交点跨过边界后进入的外部区域。

去重并处理冲突（同一交点对应两个不同外部 ID 时丢弃）。

下面给出完整的实现代码（替换 regionDetectionExact.ts 中对应的函数，并添加必要的辅助函数）。

typescript
// ========== 辅助：世界坐标转网格索引 ==========
function worldToGrid(
  x: number,
  y: number,
  xMin: number,
  yMin: number,
  stepX: number,
  stepY: number,
  resolution: number
): { i: number; j: number } | null {
  const j = Math.floor((x - xMin) / stepX);
  const i = Math.floor((y - yMin) / stepY);
  if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) {
    return { i, j };
  }
  return null;
}

// ========== 在交点周围扫描网格，确定外部区域 ID ==========
/**
 * 在给定点周围的正方形窗口内，寻找一个不属于当前区域 (rid) 且不是墙 (id !== -1) 的区域 ID。
 * 策略：收集所有候选 ID，先按出现频率排序，频率相同者按到交点的距离（网格中心距离）取最近。
 * @param point 世界坐标点（通常是图形交点）
 * @param gridData 网格数据
 * @param rid 当前内部区域 ID
 * @param radius 扫描半径（以格子为单位），默认 2
 * @returns 找到的外部区域 ID；如果未找到则返回 null
 */
function getOutsideIdAroundPoint(
  point: Point,
  gridData: GridData,
  rid: number,
  radius: number = 2
): number | null {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;
  const { i: ci, j: cj } = worldToGrid(point.x, point.y, xMin, yMin, stepX, stepY, resolution) || { i: -1, j: -1 };
  if (ci === -1) return null;

  type Candidate = { id: number; distSq: number };
  const candidates: Candidate[] = [];

  // 扫描正方形窗口
  for (let di = -radius; di <= radius; di++) {
    for (let dj = -radius; dj <= radius; dj++) {
      const ni = ci + di;
      const nj = cj + dj;
      if (ni < 0 || ni >= resolution || nj < 0 || nj >= resolution) continue;
      const nid = regionIdGrid[ni][nj];
      if (nid === rid || nid === -1) continue;

      // 计算该格子中心到交点的距离平方
      const centerX = xMin + (nj + 0.5) * stepX;
      const centerY = yMin + (ni + 0.5) * stepY;
      const dx = centerX - point.x;
      const dy = centerY - point.y;
      const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      candidates.push({ id: nid, distSq });
    }
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    // 没找到任何外部区域，扩大半径再试一次
    if (radius < 4) {
      return getOutsideIdAroundPoint(point, gridData, rid, radius + 1);
    }
    return null;
  }

  // 按 ID 分组，统计频率，并记录最小距离
  const freqMap = new Map<number, { count: number; minDistSq: number }>();
  for (const cand of candidates) {
    const existing = freqMap.get(cand.id);
    if (existing) {
      existing.count++;
      if (cand.distSq < existing.minDistSq) existing.minDistSq = cand.distSq;
    } else {
      freqMap.set(cand.id, { count: 1, minDistSq: cand.distSq });
    }
  }

  // 选出频率最高的 ID，若频率相同则选距离最近者
  let bestId: number | null = null;
  let bestCount = 0;
  let bestDistSq = Infinity;
  for (const [id, { count, minDistSq }] of freqMap.entries()) {
    if (count > bestCount || (count === bestCount && minDistSq < bestDistSq)) {
      bestCount = count;
      bestDistSq = minDistSq;
      bestId = id;
    }
  }
  return bestId;
}

// ========== 核心：收集边界点（新版本，无法线） ==========
function collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(
  regionId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  shapes: Shape[]
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;

  // 获取当前区域对象
  const region = gridData.regions.find(r => r.id === regionId);
  if (!region) return [];

  // 用于存储唯一的边界点
  const pointMap = new Map<string, BoundaryPoint>();

  // 8 个方向 (单位向量)
  const directions = [
    { x: 1, y: 0 }, { x: -1, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 1 }, { x: 0, y: -1 },
    { x: 0.7071067811865475, y: 0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: 0.7071067811865475, y: -0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: -0.7071067811865475, y: 0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: -0.7071067811865475, y: -0.7071067811865475 }
  ];

  // 遍历区域内所有网格单元
  for (const cell of region.cells) {
    const { i, j } = cell;
    const centerX = xMin + (j + 0.5) * stepX;
    const centerY = yMin + (i + 0.5) * stepY;
    const origin = { x: centerX, y: centerY };

    for (const dir of directions) {
      // 发射射线，找到最近的图形交点
      const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
      if (!hit) continue;

      const hitPoint = hit.point;
      // 通过周围网格确定外部区域 ID
      const outsideId = getOutsideIdAroundPoint(hitPoint, gridData, regionId);
      if (outsideId === null) continue;

      // 去重：用极高精度 key
      const key = `${Math.round(hitPoint.x * 1e9)}_${Math.round(hitPoint.y * 1e9)}`;
      const existing = pointMap.get(key);
      if (!existing) {
        pointMap.set(key, {
          point: hitPoint,
          insideId: regionId,
          outsideId: outsideId
        });
      } else if (existing.outsideId !== outsideId) {
        // 同一交点对应不同外部区域 → 歧义，删除该点
        pointMap.delete(key);
      }
      // 相同则无需重复添加
    }
  }

  return Array.from(pointMap.values());
}
同时需要修改 computeRegionsExact 中对 collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion 的调用方式，因为新函数不再需要传递 shapes 以外的多余参数（之前有 angleStepDeg 等）。原调用处保持不变即可。

关于 getNearestIntersection：它已经存在于 regionDetectionExact.ts 中，无需修改。

移除不再使用的函数（可选，但建议清理）：

computeNormalAtIntersection

findValidNeighborRegionId（虽然可以保留但不影响，因为新算法不再依赖它）

对性能的说明：

每个区域内每个格子向 8 个方向发射射线，每条射线会调用 getNearestIntersection（遍历所有图形求交），复杂度较高。实际应用中可以通过只处理边界格子来优化：只对那些至少有一个四连通邻居的 regionId !== regionId 的格子进行探测。但为了代码清晰，上述实现保持简单，你可以后续自行加入边界格子筛选。

交点周围的窗口扫描半径为 2（可配置），每个交点最多扫描 25 个格子，开销很小。

使用示例
在 refreshRegionCache 中调用 computeRegionsExact，它内部会调用 collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion。新实现无需修改外部接口，因此原有流程完全兼容。

验证要点
射线方向覆盖：8 个方向基本覆盖了所有可能的边界法线方向，能捕获所有边界段。

外部区域识别：对于每个交点，扫描周围网格找到的外部区域 ID 应当和直观的“边界另一侧”一致。如果交点位于多个区域的交界点，多数投票 + 距离机制会选择一个主要区域，这在实际使用中足够了。

歧义点处理：当同一个交点被不同格子、不同方向探测到，且给出不同 outsideId 时，会直接丢弃该交点，避免生成自交的多边形。你可以根据调试需要决定是否记录警告日志。

如果测试发现某些区域多边形不完整，可以尝试增加 resolution（例如 400 或 500）或增加射线方向数量（例如改为 16 方向）。













好的，以下是根据您的要求重新生成的边检验代码，专注于将每个 outsideId 分组内的点集校验为有效的开放折线或闭合环，并保留完整的点序列和端点信息，用于后续的图论环构建。

typescript
// ========== 类型定义 ==========
interface ValidEdge {
  type: 'valid_edge';
  points: Point[];      // 整条折线的点序列（有序，不闭合）
  start: Point;         // 起点坐标
  end: Point;           // 终点坐标
}

interface ValidLoop {
  type: 'valid_loop';
  points: Point[];      // 闭合环的点序列（首尾不重复，已闭合）
}

type ValidationResult = ValidEdge | ValidLoop;

// ========== 辅助函数 ==========
function distance(a: Point, b: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
}

/** 获取世界坐标所在网格的 regionId，超出边界返回 -1 */
function getGridId(p: Point, gridData: GridData): number {
  const { xMin, yMin, stepX, stepY, resolution, regionIdGrid } = gridData;
  const j = Math.floor((p.x - xMin) / stepX);
  const i = Math.floor((p.y - yMin) / stepY);
  if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) {
    return regionIdGrid[i][j];
  }
  return -1;
}

/** 基于距离阈值的点集聚类（欧氏距离） */
function clusterPoints(points: Point[], threshold: number): Point[][] {
  const used = new Set<Point>();
  const clusters: Point[][] = [];
  for (const p of points) {
    if (used.has(p)) continue;
    const cluster: Point[] = [];
    const queue = [p];
    used.add(p);
    while (queue.length) {
      const cur = queue.shift()!;
      cluster.push(cur);
      for (const other of points) {
        if (used.has(other)) continue;
        if (distance(cur, other) <= threshold) {
          used.add(other);
          queue.push(other);
        }
      }
    }
    clusters.push(cluster);
  }
  return clusters;
}

/** 将无序点集排序成连续折线（适用于几乎连续的边界） */
function orderPointsIntoPolyline(points: Point[], step: number): Point[] {
  if (points.length <= 2) return points.slice();
  const threshold = step * 1.5;
  const adj = new Map<Point, Point[]>();
  for (const p of points) adj.set(p, []);
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < points.length; j++) {
      const d = distance(points[i], points[j]);
      if (d < threshold) {
        adj.get(points[i])!.push(points[j]);
        adj.get(points[j])!.push(points[i]);
      }
    }
  }
  // 寻找端点（度为1）
  let start = points[0];
  for (const [p, nbrs] of adj) {
    if (nbrs.length === 1) { start = p; break; }
  }
  const ordered: Point[] = [];
  const visited = new Set<Point>();
  let current = start;
  while (current && !visited.has(current)) {
    ordered.push(current);
    visited.add(current);
    const nbrs = adj.get(current) || [];
    let next: Point | undefined;
    for (const n of nbrs) {
      if (!visited.has(n)) { next = n; break; }
    }
    current = next!;
  }
  return ordered;
}

/** 计算折线的合理比例（两侧分别为 regionId 和 outsideId 的线段比例） */
function computeValidRatio(
  polyline: Point[],
  regionId: number,
  outsideId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  isClosed: boolean
): number {
  if (polyline.length < 2) return 0;
  const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
  const offset = step * 0.3;
  let valid = 0, total = 0;
  const segments: [Point, Point][] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < polyline.length - 1; i++) {
    segments.push([polyline[i], polyline[i+1]]);
  }
  if (isClosed && polyline.length >= 3) {
    segments.push([polyline[polyline.length-1], polyline[0]]);
  }
  for (const [p1, p2] of segments) {
    const len = distance(p1, p2);
    if (len < 1e-9) continue;
    const mid = { x: (p1.x + p2.x) / 2, y: (p1.y + p2.y) / 2 };
    const dx = p2.x - p1.x, dy = p2.y - p1.y;
    const nx = -dy / len, ny = dx / len;
    const left = { x: mid.x + nx * offset, y: mid.y + ny * offset };
    const right = { x: mid.x - nx * offset, y: mid.y - ny * offset };
    const leftId = getGridId(left, gridData);
    const rightId = getGridId(right, gridData);
    total++;
    if ((leftId === regionId && rightId === outsideId) ||
        (leftId === outsideId && rightId === regionId)) {
      valid++;
    }
  }
  return total === 0 ? 0 : valid / total;
}

/** 计算两个点集之间的最小距离 */
function minClusterDistance(clusterA: Point[], clusterB: Point[]): number {
  let min = Infinity;
  for (const p of clusterA) {
    for (const q of clusterB) {
      const d = distance(p, q);
      if (d < min) min = d;
    }
  }
  return min;
}

/** 合并所有簇（简单 flat） */
function mergeAllClusters(clusters: Point[][]): Point[] {
  return clusters.flat();
}

// ========== 主校验函数 ==========
/**
 * 对同一个 outsideId 分组的所有边界点进行校验。
 * 返回有效的开放折线或闭合环，每个结果保留完整点序列和端点信息。
 */
export function validateSegmentGroup(
  points: Point[],
  regionId: number,
  outsideId: number,
  gridData: GridData
): ValidationResult[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return [];

  const step = Math.max(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
  const clusterThresh = step * 1.5;
  let clusters = clusterPoints(points, clusterThresh);

  // 真空检测：若存在两个簇且间距过大 → 废弃整组
  const gapThresh = step * 2.0;
  if (clusters.length >= 2) {
    let minGap = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < clusters.length; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < clusters.length; j++) {
        const d = minClusterDistance(clusters[i], clusters[j]);
        if (d < minGap) minGap = d;
      }
    }
    if (minGap > gapThresh) {
      return []; // 中间有真空，废弃
    }
    // 若间隙较小，合并所有点后重新聚类
    points = mergeAllClusters(clusters);
    clusters = clusterPoints(points, clusterThresh);
  }

  const results: ValidationResult[] = [];
  for (const cluster of clusters) {
    if (cluster.length < 3) continue;
    const polyline = orderPointsIntoPolyline(cluster, step);
    if (polyline.length < 3) continue;

    const isClosed = distance(polyline[0], polyline[polyline.length-1]) < step * 0.8;
    const ratio = computeValidRatio(polyline, regionId, outsideId, gridData, isClosed);
    if (ratio < 0.3) continue;      // 不合理，废弃
    if (ratio < 0.7) continue;      // 部分合理，为简化也废弃（可配置）

    if (isClosed) {
      // 闭合环：去掉末尾重复的起点
      const loopPoints = polyline.slice(0, -1);
      results.push({ type: 'valid_loop', points: loopPoints });
    } else {
      results.push({
        type: 'valid_edge',
        points: polyline,
        start: polyline[0],
        end: polyline[polyline.length-1]
      });
    }
  }
  return results;
}









以下是根据您的需求修改后的 regionDetectionExact.ts 完整代码。核心改进点：

修复 findAllCycles：能找出所有环，而非只找一个。

使用容差匹配端点：避免浮点误差导致匹配失败。

改进图构建：基于距离的节点去重。

合并独立环与拼接环，确保每个区域的所有闭合多边形都被返回。

typescript
import type { Point, Shape } from '../types';

// ========== 原有依赖（假设这些函数已在文件中存在或在此实现） ==========
// 注意：以下函数应已在 regionDetectionExact.ts 中定义，这里仅作引用声明
declare function computeGridRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number
): GridData;

declare function collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(
  regionId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  shapes: Shape[]
): BoundaryPoint[];

// ========== 类型定义 ==========
export interface GridData {
  regionIdGrid: number[][];
  regions: GridRegion[];
  stepX: number;
  stepY: number;
  xMin: number;
  yMin: number;
  resolution: number;
}

export interface GridRegion {
  id: number;
  cells: { i: number; j: number }[];
  bounds: { minI: number; maxI: number; minJ: number; maxJ: number };
  seed: Point;
  touchesEdge: boolean;
}

export interface BoundaryPoint {
  point: Point;
  insideId: number;
  outsideId: number;
}

// ========== 新实现：边段定义 ==========
interface EdgeSegment {
  id: string;
  points: Point[];      // 有序点列（首尾不重复，开放边首尾不重合；独立环首尾距离 < 阈值）
  start: Point;
  end: Point;
  outsideId: number;
  isClosed: boolean;    // true 表示独立环，不参与图论；false 表示开放边
}

// 容差：基于网格步长
function getEps(gridData: GridData): number {
  return Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY) * 0.1;
}

function distance(a: Point, b: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
}

// 点是否相等（容差内）
function pointsEqual(a: Point, b: Point, eps: number): boolean {
  return Math.abs(a.x - b.x) < eps && Math.abs(a.y - b.y) < eps;
}

// 聚类：基于距离连通性
function clusterPointsByDistance(points: Point[], threshold: number): Point[][] {
  const clusters: Point[][] = [];
  const used = new Set<Point>();
  for (const p of points) {
    if (used.has(p)) continue;
    const cluster: Point[] = [];
    const queue = [p];
    used.add(p);
    while (queue.length) {
      const cur = queue.shift()!;
      cluster.push(cur);
      for (const other of points) {
        if (used.has(other)) continue;
        if (distance(cur, other) <= threshold) {
          used.add(other);
          queue.push(other);
        }
      }
    }
    clusters.push(cluster);
  }
  return clusters;
}

// 将无序点集排序为连续折线（基于邻接表的最长路径）
function orderPointsToPolyline(points: Point[], threshold: number): Point[] {
  if (points.length <= 2) return points.slice();
  
  // 构建邻接表
  const adj = new Map<Point, Point[]>();
  for (const p of points) adj.set(p, []);
  for (let i = 0; i < points.length; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < points.length; j++) {
      if (distance(points[i], points[j]) <= threshold) {
        adj.get(points[i])!.push(points[j]);
        adj.get(points[j])!.push(points[i]);
      }
    }
  }
  
  // 寻找端点（度为1）
  const endpoints = points.filter(p => adj.get(p)!.length === 1);
  
  // 如果没有端点（全是度2），说明是一个闭合环
  if (endpoints.length === 0) {
    const start = points[0];
    const visited = new Set<Point>();
    const ordered: Point[] = [];
    let cur = start;
    let prev: Point | null = null;
    while (cur && !visited.has(cur)) {
      ordered.push(cur);
      visited.add(cur);
      const nbrs = adj.get(cur)!;
      const next = nbrs.find(n => n !== prev);
      if (!next) break;
      prev = cur;
      cur = next;
    }
    // 闭合环去掉最后一个重复点（保持开放形式）
    if (ordered.length > 1 && distance(ordered[0], ordered[ordered.length - 1]) < threshold * 0.8) {
      ordered.pop();
    }
    return ordered;
  }
  
  // 从第一个端点开始走最长路径
  const start = endpoints[0];
  const visited = new Set<Point>();
  const path: Point[] = [];
  let cur = start;
  let prev: Point | null = null;
  while (cur && !visited.has(cur)) {
    path.push(cur);
    visited.add(cur);
    const nbrs = adj.get(cur)!;
    const next = nbrs.find(n => n !== prev);
    if (!next) break;
    prev = cur;
    cur = next;
  }
  return path;
}

// 从 same outsideId 的点集中提取边段
function extractEdgesFromGroup(
  points: Point[],
  outsideId: number,
  gridData: GridData
): EdgeSegment[] {
  if (points.length < 2) return [];
  const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
  const clusterThreshold = step * 1.5;
  const closeThreshold = step * 0.8;
  
  const clusters = clusterPointsByDistance(points, clusterThreshold);
  const edges: EdgeSegment[] = [];
  
  for (let idx = 0; idx < clusters.length; idx++) {
    const cluster = clusters[idx];
    if (cluster.length < 2) continue;
    const ordered = orderPointsToPolyline(cluster, clusterThreshold);
    if (ordered.length < 2) continue;
    const first = ordered[0];
    const last = ordered[ordered.length - 1];
    const dist = distance(first, last);
    const naturallyClosed = dist < closeThreshold;
    
    if (naturallyClosed && ordered.length >= 3) {
      // 独立环，去掉重复的终点
      const pointsForLoop = ordered.slice(0, -1);
      if (pointsForLoop.length >= 3) {
        edges.push({
          id: `out_${outsideId}_clust_${idx}_loop`,
          points: pointsForLoop,
          start: pointsForLoop[0],
          end: pointsForLoop[pointsForLoop.length - 1],
          outsideId,
          isClosed: true,
        });
      }
    } else {
      edges.push({
        id: `out_${outsideId}_clust_${idx}_edge`,
        points: ordered,
        start: ordered[0],
        end: ordered[ordered.length - 1],
        outsideId,
        isClosed: false,
      });
    }
  }
  return edges;
}

// ========== 图拼接：找出所有闭合环 ==========
interface GraphNode {
  point: Point;
  edges: EdgeSegment[];
}

// 构建图（使用容差匹配端点）
function buildGraph(edges: EdgeSegment[], eps: number): Map<string, GraphNode> {
  const nodes: Map<string, GraphNode> = new Map();
  const getNodeKey = (p: Point): string => {
    // 找已存在的距离 eps 内的节点
    for (const [key, node] of nodes.entries()) {
      if (pointsEqual(node.point, p, eps)) return key;
    }
    // 不存在则创建新节点
    const key = `${p.x},${p.y}`;
    nodes.set(key, { point: p, edges: [] });
    return key;
  };
  
  for (const edge of edges) {
    const startKey = getNodeKey(edge.start);
    const endKey = getNodeKey(edge.end);
    nodes.get(startKey)!.edges.push(edge);
    if (startKey !== endKey) {
      nodes.get(endKey)!.edges.push(edge);
    }
  }
  return nodes;
}

// 深度优先搜索，找出一条闭合环（基于边的不重复使用）
function findOneCycleFromEdge(
  startEdge: EdgeSegment,
  nodes: Map<string, GraphNode>,
  eps: number
): { points: Point[]; edges: EdgeSegment[] } | null {
  // 记录路径上的点和边
  const pathPoints: Point[] = [];
  const pathEdges: EdgeSegment[] = [];
  const usedEdges = new Set<EdgeSegment>();
  
  // 从 startEdge 开始，确定起点方向
  const startNodeKey = (() => {
    for (const [key, node] of nodes.entries()) {
      if (pointsEqual(node.point, startEdge.start, eps)) return key;
    }
    return null;
  })();
  if (!startNodeKey) return null;
  
  // 初始化：将 startEdge 加入路径
  pathEdges.push(startEdge);
  usedEdges.add(startEdge);
  // 根据起点确定点的顺序
  if (pointsEqual(startEdge.start, nodes.get(startNodeKey)!.point, eps)) {
    pathPoints.push(...startEdge.points);
  } else {
    pathPoints.push(...[...startEdge.points].reverse());
  }
  
  let currentNodeKey = (() => {
    const lastPoint = pathPoints[pathPoints.length - 1];
    for (const [key, node] of nodes.entries()) {
      if (pointsEqual(node.point, lastPoint, eps)) return key;
    }
    return null;
  })();
  
  while (true) {
    const currentNode = nodes.get(currentNodeKey!);
    if (!currentNode) break;
    
    // 找到一条未使用的边（且不是刚刚走过的那条边的反向）
    let nextEdge: EdgeSegment | undefined;
    for (const edge of currentNode.edges) {
      if (!usedEdges.has(edge) && edge !== pathEdges[pathEdges.length - 1]) {
        nextEdge = edge;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!nextEdge) break;
    
    // 确定添加方向
    const currentPoint = currentNode.point;
    let segmentPoints: Point[];
    if (pointsEqual(nextEdge.start, currentPoint, eps)) {
      segmentPoints = nextEdge.points.slice(1);
    } else if (pointsEqual(nextEdge.end, currentPoint, eps)) {
      segmentPoints = [...nextEdge.points].reverse().slice(1);
    } else {
      break;
    }
    pathPoints.push(...segmentPoints);
    pathEdges.push(nextEdge);
    usedEdges.add(nextEdge);
    
    // 更新当前节点
    const lastPoint = pathPoints[pathPoints.length - 1];
    let nextNodeKey: string | null = null;
    for (const [key, node] of nodes.entries()) {
      if (pointsEqual(node.point, lastPoint, eps)) {
        nextNodeKey = key;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!nextNodeKey) break;
    currentNodeKey = nextNodeKey;
    
    // 检查是否回到起点（形成环）
    if (pointsEqual(pathPoints[pathPoints.length - 1], pathPoints[0], eps) && pathEdges.length > 1) {
      // 去掉最后一个重复点
      const finalPoints = pathPoints.slice(0, -1);
      if (finalPoints.length >= 3) {
        return { points: finalPoints, edges: pathEdges };
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// 找出所有环（使用边的全局集合，每次找到后移除环上的边）
function findAllCycles(edges: EdgeSegment[], eps: number): Point[][] {
  const openEdges = edges.filter(e => !e.isClosed);
  if (openEdges.length === 0) return [];
  
  const nodes = buildGraph(openEdges, eps);
  const remainingEdges = new Set(openEdges);
  const cycles: Point[][] = [];
  
  while (remainingEdges.size > 0) {
    // 取一条未使用的边作为起点
    const startEdge = Array.from(remainingEdges)[0];
    const cycle = findOneCycleFromEdge(startEdge, nodes, eps);
    if (cycle) {
      cycles.push(cycle.points);
      // 移除该环上的所有边
      for (const e of cycle.edges) {
        remainingEdges.delete(e);
      }
    } else {
      // 无法形成环，可能是孤立线段，移除它避免死循环
      remainingEdges.delete(startEdge);
    }
  }
  
  return cycles;
}

// ========== 主函数：计算所有封闭区域（每个区域包含多个环） ==========
export function computeRegionsExact(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500
): Point[][][] {
  console.log('[computeRegionsExact] 开始区域检测...');
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 共发现 ${gridData.regions.length} 个连通区域`);
  
  const mainRegions = gridData.regions.filter(r => !r.touchesEdge && r.cells.length >= 10);
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 其中主区域 ${mainRegions.length} 个`);
  
  const result: Point[][][] = [];
  const eps = getEps(gridData);
  
  for (const region of mainRegions) {
    // 收集边界点
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    if (boundaryPoints.length < 3) continue;
    
    // 按 outsideId 分组
    const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
    for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
      if (!groups.has(bp.outsideId)) groups.set(bp.outsideId, []);
      groups.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
    }
    
    // 提取所有边段
    let allEdges: EdgeSegment[] = [];
    for (const [outsideId, pts] of groups) {
      const edges = extractEdgesFromGroup(pts, outsideId, gridData);
      allEdges.push(...edges);
    }
    
    // 独立环（自然闭合）
    const independentLoops = allEdges.filter(e => e.isClosed).map(e => e.points);
    // 图拼接得到的环
    const constructedCycles = findAllCycles(allEdges, eps);
    // 合并所有环
    const allCycles = [...independentLoops, ...constructedCycles];
    
    if (allCycles.length === 0) continue;
    
    // 直接添加所有环（不区分内外）
    result.push(allCycles);
    console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 区域 ${region.id}: 共生成 ${allCycles.length} 个环`);
  }
  
  console.log(`[computeRegionsExact] 最终得到 ${result.length} 个区域（每个区域包含若干环）`);
  return result;
}

你说得对，当前的 reclusterBoundaryPointsToNewIds 只是做了距离聚类，但每个簇的点集仍然是无序的，直接扔给 buildClosedRing（极角排序）会导致排序错误，尤其是对于非凸的复杂边界。因此，真正的需求是：对同一个 outsideId 内的点，先按空间相邻关系排序成有序的折线（可能多个），然后将每个有序折线作为一个独立的片段（环）。下面我实现一个完整的流程：

对每个 outsideId 组内的点，建立邻接图（距离阈值内相连）。

找出所有连通分量（已经是 BFS 聚类，可以复用）。

对每个连通分量的点集，进行拓扑排序，得到有序点列（闭合或开放）。

为每个有序片段分配全新的唯一 ID，并保留原始 outsideId。

直接使用有序片段构建环（如果片段首尾距离小于阈值，则闭合；否则忽略或尝试连接）。

下面提供新函数和修改后的 computeRegionsExact 核心部分。

新增函数：将无序点集排序成有序折线（基于邻接图）
typescript
/**
 * 将无序的点集（属于同一个连通分量）排序成一条有序的折线（可能是闭合或开放）。
 * 通过邻接图（距离阈值内相连）寻找路径。
 * @param points - 无序点集
 * @param threshold - 距离阈值（与聚类阈值相同）
 * @returns 有序点列（若闭合，首尾不重复；若开放，返回路径）
 */
function orderPointsByAdjacency(points: Point[], threshold: number): Point[] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];
  if (points.length === 1) return points;

  // 构建邻接表：每个点连接距离 <= threshold 的其他点
  const n = points.length;
  const adj: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => []);
  const distSq = (i: number, j: number) => {
    const dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
    const dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
  };
  const threshSq = threshold * threshold;

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
      if (distSq(i, j) <= threshSq) {
        adj[i].push(j);
        adj[j].push(i);
      }
    }
  }

  // 寻找端点（度数为 1 的节点）
  const endpoints: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (adj[i].length === 1) endpoints.push(i);
    else if (adj[i].length === 0) {
      // 孤立点：返回单点列表（可能是噪声，但保留）
      return [points[i]];
    }
  }

  if (endpoints.length === 2) {
    // 开放折线：从一端走到另一端
    const start = endpoints[0];
    const path: number[] = [start];
    let prev = -1;
    let cur = start;
    while (true) {
      const nexts = adj[cur].filter(nb => nb !== prev);
      if (nexts.length === 0) break;
      const next = nexts[0];
      path.push(next);
      prev = cur;
      cur = next;
      if (cur === endpoints[1]) break;
    }
    return path.map(idx => points[idx]);
  } else if (endpoints.length === 0) {
    // 所有点度数都为 2（闭合环），从任意点开始绕一圈
    const path: number[] = [0];
    let prev = -1;
    let cur = 0;
    for (let step = 0; step < n; step++) {
      const nexts = adj[cur].filter(nb => nb !== prev);
      if (nexts.length === 0) break;
      const next = nexts[0];
      path.push(next);
      prev = cur;
      cur = next;
      if (cur === 0 && path.length > 1) break;
    }
    // 移除最后一个重复的起点
    if (path[0] === path[path.length - 1]) path.pop();
    return path.map(idx => points[idx]);
  } else {
    // 多个端点：取度数为 1 的两个端点（优先），否则退化为第一个点
    const start = endpoints[0];
    const path: number[] = [start];
    let prev = -1;
    let cur = start;
    while (true) {
      const nexts = adj[cur].filter(nb => nb !== prev);
      if (nexts.length === 0) break;
      const next = nexts[0];
      path.push(next);
      prev = cur;
      cur = next;
      if (cur === start) break;
    }
    return path.map(idx => points[idx]);
  }
}

/**
 * 一步完成：对边界点进行带 outsideId 约束的距离聚类，并对每个簇进行拓扑排序。
 * 输出：Map<newId, { points: Point[]; outsideId: number }>，其中 points 已排序。
 */
export function clusterAndOrderBoundaryPoints(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  threshold: number
): Map<number, { points: Point[]; outsideId: number }> {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return new Map();

  // 找出所有原始 outsideId 的最大值
  let maxOutsideId = -1;
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (bp.outsideId > maxOutsideId) maxOutsideId = bp.outsideId;
  }
  let nextNewId = maxOutsideId + 1;
  if (nextNewId < 1) nextNewId = 1;

  const n = boundaryPoints.length;
  const visited = new Array(n).fill(false);
  const result = new Map<number, { points: Point[]; outsideId: number }>();

  const distSq = (i: number, j: number) => {
    const dx = boundaryPoints[i].point.x - boundaryPoints[j].point.x;
    const dy = boundaryPoints[i].point.y - boundaryPoints[j].point.y;
    return dx * dx + dy * dy;
  };
  const threshSq = threshold * threshold;

  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (visited[i]) continue;
    const queue: number[] = [i];
    visited[i] = true;
    const clusterIndices: number[] = [i];
    const outsideId = boundaryPoints[i].outsideId;

    // BFS 收集相同 outsideId 且空间连通的点
    while (queue.length) {
      const cur = queue.shift()!;
      for (let j = 0; j < n; j++) {
        if (!visited[j] && boundaryPoints[j].outsideId === outsideId && distSq(cur, j) <= threshSq) {
          visited[j] = true;
          queue.push(j);
          clusterIndices.push(j);
        }
      }
    }

    if (clusterIndices.length < 3) continue;

    // 提取点集
    const clusterPoints = clusterIndices.map(idx => boundaryPoints[idx].point);
    // 排序
    const ordered = orderPointsByAdjacency(clusterPoints, threshold);
    if (ordered.length >= 3) {
      result.set(nextNewId++, { points: ordered, outsideId });
    }
  }

  console.log(`[clusterAndOrderBoundaryPoints] 得到 ${result.size} 个有序片段`);
  return result;
}
修改 computeRegionsExact 中的调用
在 computeRegionsExact 中，替换原来的逻辑：

typescript
// 原来的代码：
// const groupsByOutsideId = groupBoundaryPointsByOutsideId(boundaryPoints);
// const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
// const threshold = step * 1.5;
// const reclustered = reclusterBoundaryPointsToNewIds(groupsByOutsideId, threshold);
//
// const rings = [];
// for (const { points: pts, originalOutsideId } of reclustered.values()) {
//   const ring = buildClosedRing(pts);
//   ...

// 新代码：
const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
const threshold = step * 1.5;
const orderedFragments = clusterAndOrderBoundaryPoints(boundaryPoints, threshold);

const rings: { points: Point[]; outsideId: number; area: number }[] = [];
for (const { points: ordered, outsideId } of orderedFragments.values()) {
  // 检查首尾距离，如果很近（< threshold * 0.8）则闭合
  const closed = Math.hypot(ordered[0].x - ordered[ordered.length - 1].x,
                           ordered[0].y - ordered[ordered.length - 1].y) < threshold * 0.8;
  let ringPoints = ordered;
  if (!closed) {
    // 如果不闭合，可以尝试首尾相连强制闭合，或者丢弃（这里选择强制闭合）
    ringPoints = [...ordered, ordered[0]];
  }
  const area = polygonArea(ringPoints);
  // 过滤面积过小的环（噪声）
  if (ringPoints.length >= 3 && Math.abs(area) > 1e-6) {
    rings.push({ points: ringPoints, outsideId, area });
  }
}
这样，每个片段都得到了正确的空间顺序，不再依赖极角排序。然后后续的外环/内环筛选逻辑保持不变。

















我们重新整理完整代码，保证 outsideId 被替换为从 1 开始递增的全新唯一 ID，且每个连通分量排序后得到有序折线，并在调试绘制中正确显示。

1. 更新 regionDetectionExact.ts —— 添加排序聚类函数并修改相关逻辑
typescript
// ========== 在 regionDetectionExact.ts 中添加或替换以下内容 ==========

// 辅助函数：将无序点集排序成有序折线（基于邻接图）
function orderPointsByAdjacency(points: Point[], threshold: number): Point[] {
  if (points.length <= 1) return points;
  const n = points.length;
  const adj: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => []);
  const threshSq = threshold * threshold;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
      const dx = points[i].x - points[j].x;
      const dy = points[i].y - points[j].y;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= threshSq) {
        adj[i].push(j);
        adj[j].push(i);
      }
    }
  }

  // 寻找端点（度数为 1 或 0）
  let start = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    if (adj[i].length === 1) { start = i; break; }
    if (adj[i].length === 0) return [points[i]];
  }

  const path: number[] = [start];
  let prev = -1;
  let cur = start;
  while (true) {
    const nexts = adj[cur].filter(nb => nb !== prev);
    if (nexts.length === 0) break;
    const next = nexts[0];
    path.push(next);
    prev = cur;
    cur = next;
    if (cur === start && path.length > 1) break; // 闭合环
  }
  // 如果首尾相同且路径长度>2，说明是闭合环，去掉末尾重复的起点
  if (path.length > 2 && points[path[0]] === points[path[path.length-1]]) path.pop();
  return path.map(idx => points[idx]);
}

/**
 * 对边界点按空间邻接关系排序并聚类，为每个连续片段分配新ID
 * @param boundaryPoints 原始边界点数组
 * @param threshold 距离阈值（通常为网格步长 * 1.5）
 * @returns 新的边界点数组，其中 outsideId 被替换为片段唯一ID（从1开始递增），insideId 不变
 */
export function sortAndClusterBoundaryPoints(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  threshold: number
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return [];

  // 1. 按原始 outsideId 分组
  const groupsByOriginalId = new Map<number, BoundaryPoint[]>();
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (!groupsByOriginalId.has(bp.outsideId)) {
      groupsByOriginalId.set(bp.outsideId, []);
    }
    groupsByOriginalId.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp);
  }

  const newBoundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[] = [];
  let nextNewId = 1; // 新ID从1开始，非0，不与任何原有ID冲突

  for (const [, pointsInGroup] of groupsByOriginalId) {
    const rawPoints = pointsInGroup.map(bp => bp.point);
    const n = rawPoints.length;
    if (n < 3) continue; // 点太少，忽略

    // 2. 构建邻接矩阵，找出连通分量
    const adjLocal: number[][] = Array.from({ length: n }, () => []);
    const threshSq = threshold * threshold;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
        const dx = rawPoints[i].x - rawPoints[j].x;
        const dy = rawPoints[i].y - rawPoints[j].y;
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy <= threshSq) {
          adjLocal[i].push(j);
          adjLocal[j].push(i);
        }
      }
    }

    const visited = new Array(n).fill(false);
    const components: number[][] = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      if (!visited[i]) {
        const queue: number[] = [i];
        visited[i] = true;
        const comp: number[] = [i];
        while (queue.length) {
          const cur = queue.shift()!;
          for (const nb of adjLocal[cur]) {
            if (!visited[nb]) {
              visited[nb] = true;
              queue.push(nb);
              comp.push(nb);
            }
          }
        }
        components.push(comp);
      }
    }

    // 3. 对每个连通分量排序，生成有序片段
    for (const comp of components) {
      if (comp.length < 3) continue; // 忽略噪声片段
      const compPoints = comp.map(idx => rawPoints[idx]);
      const orderedPoints = orderPointsByAdjacency(compPoints, threshold);
      if (orderedPoints.length < 3) continue;

      const newId = nextNewId++;
      for (let idx = 0; idx < orderedPoints.length; idx++) {
        const pt = orderedPoints[idx];
        // 找到原始边界点中对应坐标的点（允许小误差）
        const origBp = pointsInGroup.find(bp => Math.hypot(bp.point.x - pt.x, bp.point.y - pt.y) < 1e-6);
        if (origBp) {
          newBoundaryPoints.push({
            point: pt,
            insideId: origBp.insideId,
            outsideId: newId,
          });
        }
      }
    }
  }

  console.log(`[sortAndClusterBoundaryPoints] 生成了 ${nextNewId - 1} 个有序片段`);
  return newBoundaryPoints;
}

// ========== 修改 computeRegionsExact 中的相关部分 ==========
// 找到 computeRegionsExact 函数，将其中的边界点处理替换为以下代码：

export function computeRegionsExact(
  shapes: Shape[],
