        firstRadial: radEnd,
        lastRadial: ring.lastRadial,
      };
      return { success: true, newRing };
    }
    return { success: false, newRing: null };
  }

  // 辅助函数：检查环是否闭合（首尾满足双重阈值）
  function isRingClosed(ring: OpenRing): boolean {
    if (ring.segIndices.length === 0) return false;
    return meetsThreshold(ring.firstPoint, ring.lastPoint, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold) &&
           Math.abs(ring.firstRadial - ring.lastRadial) < radialThreshold;
  }

  // 辅助函数：根据 OpenRing 构建最终点集
  function buildRingPoints(ring: OpenRing): Point[] {
    const points: Point[] = [];
    // 我们存储了片段索引顺序，但方向信息丢失了。需要更精细的存储。
    // 简化：在 tryAttachToRing 时记录每个片段的方向。改为存储带方向的片段。
    // 这里为了代码清晰，重新设计方向存储。
    // 实际实现时，可以用一个数组存储 { segIdx, reversed }。
    // 由于上面 tryAttachToRing 的四种情况，我们可以记录每个片段相对于环的方向。
    // 为避免过度复杂，我在最终实现中使用方向数组。请参考最终函数实现。
    return points; // 占位
  }

  // 由于方向管理复杂，我们重新实现一个更简洁的版本：使用双向链表的思路。
  // 实际代码中，我会提供一个完整可运行的实现。

  // 下面给出最终稳定实现：
}

// 最终稳定实现（简化但完整）
export function segmentAndBuildRings(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Point[][] {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return [];

  // 1. 按原始 outsideId 分组
  const byOriginalId = new Map<number, Point[]>();
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (!byOriginalId.has(bp.outsideId)) byOriginalId.set(bp.outsideId, []);
    byOriginalId.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
  }

  // 2. 计算全局重心（所有边界点的总重心）
  let globalCx = 0, globalCy = 0;
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    globalCx += bp.point.x;
    globalCy += bp.point.y;
  }
  const globalCentroid = { x: globalCx / boundaryPoints.length, y: globalCy / boundaryPoints.length };

  // 3. 对每个原始组进行第一次分组，收集所有片段
  const allSegments: { points: Point[]; start: Point; end: Point; closed: boolean }[] = [];
  for (const [_, pts] of byOriginalId) {
    // 计算该组的重心（用于内部径向比较）
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of pts) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    const groupCentroid = { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
    const segs = groupIntoSegments(pts, groupCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
    allSegments.push(...segs);
  }

  // 4. 第二次分组：使用全局重心进行片段间连接
  // 存储每个片段的方向（正向/反向）以及是否已使用
  interface SegmentWithDir {
    segIdx: number;
    reversed: boolean; // true 表示点集需要反向
  }

  // 构建片段端点映射
  type EndpointKey = string;
  const endpointToSegments = new Map<EndpointKey, { segIdx: number; isStart: boolean }[]>();
  for (let i = 0; i < allSegments.length; i++) {
    const seg = allSegments[i];
    const startKey = `${seg.start.x.toFixed(9)},${seg.start.y.toFixed(9)}`;
    const endKey = `${seg.end.x.toFixed(9)},${seg.end.y.toFixed(9)}`;
    if (!endpointToSegments.has(startKey)) endpointToSegments.set(startKey, []);
    if (!endpointToSegments.has(endKey)) endpointToSegments.set(endKey, []);
    endpointToSegments.get(startKey)!.push({ segIdx: i, isStart: true });
    endpointToSegments.get(endKey)!.push({ segIdx: i, isStart: false });
  }

  const usedSegmentsSet = new Set<number>();
  const rings: Point[][] = [];

  // 贪婪连接：从任意未使用的片段开始
  for (let startIdx = 0; startIdx < allSegments.length; startIdx++) {
    if (usedSegmentsSet.has(startIdx)) continue;

    // 开始构建一个新环
    const ringIndices: number[] = [startIdx];
    const ringReversed: boolean[] = [false]; // 初始正向
    let currentEndpoint = allSegments[startIdx].end; // 当前环的末端点
    let currentRadial = radialDist(currentEndpoint, globalCentroid);
    let closed = false;
    let currentSegIdx = startIdx;
    usedSegmentsSet.add(startIdx);

    // 不断查找下一个片段
    while (!closed) {
      const key = `${currentEndpoint.x.toFixed(9)},${currentEndpoint.y.toFixed(9)}`;
      const candidates = endpointToSegments.get(key) || [];
      let found = false;

      for (const cand of candidates) {
        if (cand.segIdx === currentSegIdx) continue; // 不能是自己
        if (usedSegmentsSet.has(cand.segIdx)) continue;

        const nextSeg = allSegments[cand.segIdx];
        // 检查当前端点与候选片段的哪个端点匹配
        const matchStart = meetsThreshold(currentEndpoint, nextSeg.start, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
        const matchEnd = meetsThreshold(currentEndpoint, nextSeg.end, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);

        if (matchStart || matchEnd) {
          // 判断是否需要反向
          let reversed = false;
          let newEndpoint: Point;
          let newRadial: number;
          if (matchStart) {
            // 当前端与下一片段起点匹配，正向连接
            newEndpoint = nextSeg.end;
            newRadial = radialDist(newEndpoint, globalCentroid);
            reversed = false;
          } else {
            // 当前端与下一片段终点匹配，需要反向连接
            newEndpoint = nextSeg.start;
            newRadial = radialDist(newEndpoint, globalCentroid);
            reversed = true;
          }

          // 检查径向距差
          if (Math.abs(currentRadial - newRadial) >= radialThreshold) continue;

          // 连接成功
          ringIndices.push(cand.segIdx);
          ringReversed.push(reversed);
          usedSegmentsSet.add(cand.segIdx);
          currentEndpoint = newEndpoint;
          currentRadial = newRadial;
          currentSegIdx = cand.segIdx;
          found = true;

          // 检查是否闭合：当前端点与环的起始点是否满足条件
          const firstSeg = allSegments[ringIndices[0]];
          const firstPoint = ringReversed[0] ? firstSeg.end : firstSeg.start;
          if (meetsThreshold(currentEndpoint, firstPoint, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold) &&
              Math.abs(currentRadial - radialDist(firstPoint, globalCentroid)) < radialThreshold) {
            closed = true;
          }
          break;
        }
      }

      if (!found) break; // 无法继续连接
    }

    if (closed && ringIndices.length >= 2) {
      // 构建环的点集
      const ringPoints: Point[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < ringIndices.length; i++) {
        const seg = allSegments[ringIndices[i]];
        const reversed = ringReversed[i];
        const pointsToAdd = reversed ? [...seg.points].reverse() : seg.points;
        if (i === 0) {
          ringPoints.push(...pointsToAdd);
        } else {
          // 避免重复添加连接点（第一个点已经在前一段的末尾）
          ringPoints.push(...pointsToAdd.slice(1));
        }
      }
      // 检查首尾是否重复
      const firstP = ringPoints[0];
      const lastP = ringPoints[ringPoints.length - 1];
      if (Math.hypot(firstP.x - lastP.x, firstP.y - lastP.y) > 1e-6) {
        ringPoints.push(firstP);
      }
      rings.push(ringPoints);
    }
  }

  // 处理自身闭合的片段（未参与连接且自身 closed 为 true）
  for (let i = 0; i < allSegments.length; i++) {
    if (!usedSegmentsSet.has(i) && allSegments[i].closed && allSegments[i].points.length >= 3) {
      const ring = [...allSegments[i].points];
      const first = ring[0];
      const last = ring[ring.length - 1];
      if (Math.hypot(first.x - last.x, first.y - last.y) > 1e-6) {
        ring.push(first);
      }
      rings.push(ring);
    }
  }

  // 过滤点数小于3的环
  return rings.filter(ring => ring.length >= 3);
}












以下是完整的重新实现，解决了自身闭合检测不可靠和环构建贪心策略不完整的问题。

typescript
// ========== 辅助函数 ==========
function radialDist(p: Point, centroid: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(p.x - centroid.x, p.y - centroid.y);
}

function meetsThreshold(
  a: Point,
  b: Point,
  centroid: Point,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): boolean {
  const euclidean = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
  if (euclidean >= distThreshold) return false;
  const radialA = radialDist(a, centroid);
  const radialB = radialDist(b, centroid);
  return Math.abs(radialA - radialB) < radialThreshold;
}

// ========== 第一次分组：原始 outsideId 组内重分组 ==========
interface Segment {
  id: number;
  points: Point[];
  start: Point;
  end: Point;
  closed: boolean;      // 自身是否已经闭合（首尾满足阈值）
}

function groupIntoSegments(
  points: Point[],
  centroid: Point | null,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Segment[] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];

  // 计算重心（若未提供）
  if (!centroid) {
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of points) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    centroid = { x: cx / points.length, y: cy / points.length };
  }

  // 极角排序
  const sorted = [...points];
  sorted.sort((a, b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - centroid!.y, a.x - centroid!.x);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - centroid!.y, b.x - centroid!.x);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  // 动态贪婪匹配
  const segments: { points: Point[]; last: Point }[] = [];
  for (const p of sorted) {
    let bestIdx = -1;
    let bestDist = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
      const last = segments[i].last;
      if (meetsThreshold(last, p, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
        const d = Math.hypot(last.x - p.x, last.y - p.y);
        if (d < bestDist) {
          bestDist = d;
          bestIdx = i;
        }
      }
    }
    if (bestIdx !== -1) {
      segments[bestIdx].points.push(p);
      segments[bestIdx].last = p;
    } else {
      segments.push({ points: [p], last: p });
    }
  }

  // 构建输出，并判断自身闭合（点数≥3且首尾满足阈值）
  let nextId = 0;
  const result: Segment[] = [];
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.points.length === 0) continue;
    const start = seg.points[0];
    const end = seg.points[seg.points.length - 1];
    let closed = false;
    if (seg.points.length >= 3 && meetsThreshold(start, end, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
      closed = true;
    }
    result.push({ id: nextId++, points: seg.points, start, end, closed });
  }
  return result;
}

// ========== 第二次分组：片段组合成环（双向延伸，完整验证） ==========
interface RingBuildState {
  segments: Segment[];          // 已使用的片段（按顺序）
  reversed: boolean[];          // 每个片段是否反向
  firstPoint: Point;            // 环的当前起点
  lastPoint: Point;             // 环的当前终点
  firstRadial: number;          // 起点的径向距
  lastRadial: number;           // 终点的径向距
}

function buildRingsFromSegmentsV2(
  segments: Segment[],
  globalCentroid: Point,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Point[][] {
  if (segments.length === 0) return [];

  // 构建端点索引映射
  type EndpointKey = string;
  const endpointMap = new Map<EndpointKey, { seg: Segment; isStart: boolean }[]>();
  for (const seg of segments) {
    const startKey = `${seg.start.x.toFixed(9)},${seg.start.y.toFixed(9)}`;
    const endKey = `${seg.end.x.toFixed(9)},${seg.end.y.toFixed(9)}`;
    if (!endpointMap.has(startKey)) endpointMap.set(startKey, []);
    if (!endpointMap.has(endKey)) endpointMap.set(endKey, []);
    endpointMap.get(startKey)!.push({ seg, isStart: true });
    endpointMap.get(endKey)!.push({ seg, isStart: false });
  }

  const used = new Set<Segment>();
  const rings: Point[][] = [];

  // 尝试从任意未使用的片段开始构建环
  for (const startSeg of segments) {
    if (used.has(startSeg)) continue;

    // 初始化环状态，尝试从起始片段两端扩展
    // 我们维护一个状态机，允许从环的头或尾连接新片段
    // 使用队列存储待尝试的状态（实现双向BFS式的连接）
    // 但为了简单且可靠，采用递归回溯：尝试所有可能的连接顺序，直到闭合或无法继续
    // 这里采用深度优先搜索，最多片段数量有限，性能可接受

    type State = RingBuildState;
    const initState: State = {
      segments: [startSeg],
      reversed: [false],
      firstPoint: startSeg.start,
      lastPoint: startSeg.end,
      firstRadial: radialDist(startSeg.start, globalCentroid),
      lastRadial: radialDist(startSeg.end, globalCentroid),
    };

    let bestRing: State | null = null;

    // 深度优先搜索
    function dfs(state: State): void {
      // 检查是否闭合
      const isClosed = meetsThreshold(state.firstPoint, state.lastPoint, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold) &&
                       Math.abs(state.firstRadial - state.lastRadial) < radialThreshold;
      if (isClosed && state.segments.length >= 2) {
        // 找到闭合环，选择点数最多的（更完整）
        if (!bestRing || state.segments.length > bestRing.segments.length) {
          bestRing = state;
        }
        return;
      }

      // 尝试连接新片段到环头或环尾
      const candidates: { seg: Segment; attachToHead: boolean; reversed: boolean; newFirstPoint: Point; newLastPoint: Point }[] = [];

      // 连接到环尾
      const tailKey = `${state.lastPoint.x.toFixed(9)},${state.lastPoint.y.toFixed(9)}`;
      const tailMatches = endpointMap.get(tailKey) || [];
      for (const match of tailMatches) {
        if (used.has(match.seg)) continue;
        if (state.segments.includes(match.seg)) continue; // 避免循环
        // 检查连接条件
        const connectPoint = match.isStart ? match.seg.start : match.seg.end;
        if (!meetsThreshold(state.lastPoint, connectPoint, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) continue;
        const newRadial = radialDist(connectPoint, globalCentroid);
        if (Math.abs(state.lastRadial - newRadial) >= radialThreshold) continue;

        // 确定连接后新片段的走向
        let reversed: boolean;
        let newLastPoint: Point;
        if (match.isStart) {
          // 连接点 == 片段的起点 → 正向使用片段，新尾为片段终点
          reversed = false;
          newLastPoint = match.seg.end;
        } else {
          // 连接点 == 片段的终点 → 反向使用片段，新尾为片段起点
          reversed = true;
          newLastPoint = match.seg.start;
        }
        candidates.push({
          seg: match.seg,
          attachToHead: false,
          reversed,
          newFirstPoint: state.firstPoint,
          newLastPoint,
        });
      }

      // 连接到环头
      const headKey = `${state.firstPoint.x.toFixed(9)},${state.firstPoint.y.toFixed(9)}`;
      const headMatches = endpointMap.get(headKey) || [];
      for (const match of headMatches) {
        if (used.has(match.seg)) continue;
        if (state.segments.includes(match.seg)) continue;
        const connectPoint = match.isStart ? match.seg.start : match.seg.end;
        if (!meetsThreshold(state.firstPoint, connectPoint, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) continue;
        const newRadial = radialDist(connectPoint, globalCentroid);
        if (Math.abs(state.firstRadial - newRadial) >= radialThreshold) continue;

        let reversed: boolean;
        let newFirstPoint: Point;
        if (match.isStart) {
          // 连接点 == 片段起点 → 反向使用片段（新头为片段终点）
          reversed = true;
          newFirstPoint = match.seg.end;
        } else {
          // 连接点 == 片段终点 → 正向使用片段（新头为片段起点）
          reversed = false;
          newFirstPoint = match.seg.start;
        }
        candidates.push({
          seg: match.seg,
          attachToHead: true,
          reversed,
          newFirstPoint,
          newLastPoint: state.lastPoint,
        });
      }

      // 尝试每个候选，优先选择使得径向距更连续的
      for (const cand of candidates) {
        if (used.has(cand.seg)) continue;
        used.add(cand.seg);
        const newState: State = {
          segments: cand.attachToHead ? [cand.seg, ...state.segments] : [...state.segments, cand.seg],
          reversed: cand.attachToHead ? [cand.reversed, ...state.reversed] : [...state.reversed, cand.reversed],
          firstPoint: cand.newFirstPoint,
          lastPoint: cand.newLastPoint,
          firstRadial: radialDist(cand.newFirstPoint, globalCentroid),
          lastRadial: radialDist(cand.newLastPoint, globalCentroid),
        };
        dfs(newState);
        used.delete(cand.seg);
      }
    }

    dfs(initState);

    if (bestRing) {
      // 构建环的多边形点集
      const ringPoints: Point[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < bestRing.segments.length; i++) {
        const seg = bestRing.segments[i];
        const rev = bestRing.reversed[i];
        const pts = rev ? [...seg.points].reverse() : seg.points;
        if (i === 0) {
          ringPoints.push(...pts);
        } else {
          // 避免重复连接点
          ringPoints.push(...pts.slice(1));
        }
      }
      // 闭合环
      const first = ringPoints[0];
      const last = ringPoints[ringPoints.length - 1];
      if (Math.hypot(first.x - last.x, first.y - last.y) > 1e-6) {
        ringPoints.push(first);
      }
      // 验证多边形点数至少3
      if (ringPoints.length >= 3) {
        rings.push(ringPoints);
        // 标记所有使用的片段为已使用
        for (const seg of bestRing.segments) {
          used.add(seg);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // 处理自身闭合的片段（未参与任何环且自身标记closed）
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (!used.has(seg) && seg.closed && seg.points.length >= 3) {
      const ring = [...seg.points];
      const first = ring[0];
      const last = ring[ring.length - 1];
      if (Math.hypot(first.x - last.x, first.y - last.y) > 1e-6) {
        ring.push(first);
      }
      rings.push(ring);
      used.add(seg);
    }
  }

  // 统一环的方向：外环逆时针（正面积），内环顺时针（负面积）
  function polygonArea(points: Point[]): number {
    let area = 0;
    const n = points.length;
    for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
      area += points[j].x * points[i].y - points[j].y * points[i].x;
    }
    return area / 2;
  }
  for (const ring of rings) {
    const area = polygonArea(ring);
    if (area > 0) {
      // 逆时针 -> 保持
    } else if (area < 0) {
      // 顺时针 -> 反转
      ring.reverse();
    }
    // 面积为零的环应该被过滤掉
  }

  // 过滤面积接近0或点数不足的环
  return rings.filter(ring => ring.length >= 3 && Math.abs(polygonArea(ring)) > 1e-6);
}

// ========== 对外主函数 ==========
export function segmentAndBuildRings(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Point[][] {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return [];

  // 1. 按原始 outsideId 分组
  const byOriginalId = new Map<number, Point[]>();
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (!byOriginalId.has(bp.outsideId)) byOriginalId.set(bp.outsideId, []);
    byOriginalId.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
  }

  // 2. 计算全局重心
  let gcx = 0, gcy = 0;
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    gcx += bp.point.x;
    gcy += bp.point.y;
  }
  const globalCentroid = { x: gcx / boundaryPoints.length, y: gcy / boundaryPoints.length };

  // 3. 对每个原始组进行第一次分组，收集所有片段
  let allSegments: Segment[] = [];
  for (const [_, pts] of byOriginalId) {
    // 该组自己的重心（用于内部片段划分）
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of pts) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    const groupCentroid = { x: cx / pts.length, y: cy / pts.length };
    const segs = groupIntoSegments(pts, groupCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
    allSegments.push(...segs);
  }

  // 4. 第二次分组：组合成环
  return buildRingsFromSegmentsV2(allSegments, globalCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
}




















算法思想复述
你设计的 groupIntoSegments 函数的核心思想是：将属于同一个原始 outsideId 的无序边界点集，通过双重阈值约束，聚合成若干个有序的连续片段（Segment），并判断每个片段是否自身首尾闭合。

关键步骤
重心计算
使用该组所有点的平均值作为局部重心（centroid），用于后续极角排序和径向距计算。

极角排序
将所有点按相对于重心的极角（atan2(y - cy, x - cx)）排序。因为边界点通常围绕重心近似环状分布，极角排序为贪婪匹配提供了自然的顺序基础。

贪婪匹配构建片段
遍历排序后的每个点 p，检查它与已有片段最后一个点是否满足双重阈值条件：

欧氏距离 < distThreshold（确保空间相邻）

径向距差 |radial(p) - radial(last)| < radialThreshold（确保到重心的距离变化平缓，避免跨越不同的径向层次）

选择所有满足条件的片段中欧氏距离最小的一个，将 p 追加到该片段末尾；若没有符合条件的片段，则新建一个以 p 开头的片段。

这个策略能够将原本因为极角排序而“断裂”但实际上连续的边界点重新连接成正确的顺序片段。

自身闭合检测
如果一个片段点数 ≥ 3，并且其首点与尾点也满足双重阈值条件，则将该片段标记为 closed = true。这样的片段已经形成一个完整的闭合环（例如某些小孔洞或独立闭合区域）。

与原有 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle 的区别
原有函数只是简单地根据极角排序后的相邻点进行分裂（splitByPolarAngleAndDistance），缺乏对片段生长过程的精细控制，且没有“片段内自身闭合检测”。新算法通过动态匹配和双向阈值约束，能够更鲁棒地将无序点集还原为正确的连续线段，并为后续的环拼接提供高质量的“积木块”。

完美兼容替换版本
下面给出完全替换 regionDetectionExact.ts 中原有边界点聚类逻辑的代码。
我只修改了与第一次分组相关的部分，保留了后续环构建（buildClosedRingsByDistanceGrouping 等）不变，确保你的第二个分组函数（目前暂不处理）仍能基于新的片段点集正常工作。

typescript
// ========== 新增：双重阈值辅助函数 ==========
function radialDist(p: Point, centroid: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(p.x - centroid.x, p.y - centroid.y);
}

function meetsThreshold(
  a: Point,
  b: Point,
  centroid: Point,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): boolean {
  const euclidean = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
  if (euclidean >= distThreshold) return false;
  const radialA = radialDist(a, centroid);
  const radialB = radialDist(b, centroid);
  return Math.abs(radialA - radialB) < radialThreshold;
}

// ========== 第一次分组：将无序点集构建为有序片段 ==========
interface Segment {
  id: number;
  points: Point[];
  start: Point;
  end: Point;
  closed: boolean;      // 自身是否已经闭合（首尾满足阈值）
}

function groupIntoSegments(
  points: Point[],
  centroid: Point | null,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Segment[] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];

  // 若未提供重心，则计算该组点集的重心
  if (!centroid) {
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of points) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    centroid = { x: cx / points.length, y: cy / points.length };
  }

  // 极角排序
  const sorted = [...points];
  sorted.sort((a, b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - centroid!.y, a.x - centroid!.x);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - centroid!.y, b.x - centroid!.x);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  // 贪婪匹配构建片段
  const segments: { points: Point[]; last: Point }[] = [];
  for (const p of sorted) {
    let bestIdx = -1;
    let bestDist = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
      const last = segments[i].last;
      if (meetsThreshold(last, p, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
        const d = Math.hypot(last.x - p.x, last.y - p.y);
        if (d < bestDist) {
          bestDist = d;
          bestIdx = i;
        }
      }
    }
    if (bestIdx !== -1) {
      segments[bestIdx].points.push(p);
      segments[bestIdx].last = p;
    } else {
      segments.push({ points: [p], last: p });
    }
  }

  // 构建输出 Segment，并检测自身闭合
  let nextId = 0;
  const result: Segment[] = [];
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.points.length === 0) continue;
    const start = seg.points[0];
    const end = seg.points[seg.points.length - 1];
    let closed = false;
    if (seg.points.length >= 3 && meetsThreshold(start, end, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
      closed = true;
    }
    result.push({ id: nextId++, points: seg.points, start, end, closed });
  }
  return result;
}

/**
 * 新版的边界点重聚类函数（替换原有的 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle）
 * 使用 groupIntoSegments 将每个原始 outsideId 的点集划分为有序片段，
 * 并为每个片段内的点分配一个新的 outsideId（片段 ID），返回重新分配后的 BoundaryPoint 列表。
 *
 * @param boundaryPoints 原始边界点（包含 insideId/outsideId）
 * @param distThreshold  欧氏距离阈值（建议取网格步长的 1.5~2 倍）
 * @param radialThreshold 径向距差阈值（建议取网格步长的 0.5~1 倍）
 */
export function reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngleV2(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return [];

  // 按原始 outsideId 分组
  const byOriginalId = new Map<number, { point: Point; insideId: number }[]>();
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (!byOriginalId.has(bp.outsideId)) {
      byOriginalId.set(bp.outsideId, []);
    }
    byOriginalId.get(bp.outsideId)!.push({ point: bp.point, insideId: bp.insideId });
  }

  const newBoundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[] = [];
  let nextSegmentId = 1;

  // 对每个原始 outsideId 组分别处理
  for (const [origId, ptsWithInfo] of byOriginalId) {
    const points = ptsWithInfo.map(item => item.point);
    // 计算该组的重心（用于片段内部分组）
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of points) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    const groupCentroid = { x: cx / points.length, y: cy / points.length };

    const segments = groupIntoSegments(points, groupCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
    // 为每个片段分配一个新的 outsideId
    for (const seg of segments) {
      const newId = nextSegmentId++;
      for (const pt of seg.points) {
        // 找到原始 insideId（该点可能由多个原始边界点重复？但 groupIntoSegments 输入已去重，直接取第一个匹配即可）
        const original = ptsWithInfo.find(item => Math.hypot(item.point.x - pt.x, item.point.y - pt.y) < 1e-6);
        const insideId = original ? original.insideId : boundaryPoints[0].insideId; // fallback
        newBoundaryPoints.push({
          point: pt,
          insideId: insideId,
          outsideId: newId,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  return newBoundaryPoints;
}
如何替换原有代码
将上述代码添加到 regionDetectionExact.ts 文件末尾（或替换原有的 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle 函数及其辅助函数）。

修改 getDebugRegions 函数中的调用：
找到原本调用 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle 的地方（大约是计算 reclusteredPoints 的那一行），替换为：

typescript
// 原来的代码（示例）：
// const reclusteredPoints = reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle(boundaryPoints, 2.5, distanceThresholdFactor);

// 替换为：
const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
const distThreshold = step * 1.8;      // 建议值，可根据实际情况调整
const radialThreshold = step * 0.6;    // 建议值
const reclusteredPoints = reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngleV2(boundaryPoints, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
注意：distanceThresholdFactor 原本作为 gapFactor 传给旧函数，新函数不再需要该参数；你需要根据网格步长 step 计算两个阈值。可以在 getDebugRegions 内部获取 step（gridData.stepX 或 gridData.stepY）。如果希望保持参数传递方式不变，也可以将 distThreshold 和 radialThreshold 作为额外参数传入，并修改函数签名，但这会破坏兼容性。上面给出的方式是在 getDebugRegions 内部动态计算，对原有接口无侵入。

后续代码保持不变：
uniqueBoundaryPoints、ringsFromSegments、outsideIdEndpoints 等仍然基于新的 reclusteredPoints 运行，因为新函数返回的 BoundaryPoint[] 结构与原来完全一致（只是 outsideId 被重新编号为片段ID）。

可选：删除旧函数
如果确定不再需要旧的 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle 和 splitByPolarAngleAndDistance，可以将其删除或注释，以保持代码整洁。













你的报错主要原因是：在 regionDetectionExact.ts 中，buildClosedRingsByDistanceGrouping 函数内部的对象字面量定义缺少正确的语法结构（可能由于中文注释乱码导致换行或冒号被错误解析），并且文件末尾多了一个多余的 }。另外，新引入的 reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle 函数虽然逻辑正确，但若原文件存在括号不匹配，也会导致链式错误。

下面我给出完全修复并整合了新算法的 regionDetectionExact.ts 完整代码。你直接整体替换原文件即可。

typescript
import type { Point, Shape } from '../types';

// ========== 1. 光栅化：将图形边缘画到网格上 ==========
function rasterizeLine(
  x0: number, y0: number,
  x1: number, y1: number,
  stepX: number, stepY: number,
  xMin: number, yMin: number,
  resolution: number,
  wallGrid: boolean[][]
): void {
  const toGrid = (x: number, y: number): [number, number] => {
    let i = Math.floor((y - yMin) / stepY);
    let j = Math.floor((x - xMin) / stepX);
    i = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, i));
    j = Math.max(0, Math.min(resolution - 1, j));
    return [i, j];
  };
  const [i0, j0] = toGrid(x0, y0);
  const [i1, j1] = toGrid(x1, y1);
  let i = i0, j = j0;
  const di = Math.abs(i1 - i0);
  const dj = Math.abs(j1 - j0);
  const si = i0 < i1 ? 1 : -1;
  const sj = j0 < j1 ? 1 : -1;
  let err = di - dj;
  while (true) {
    if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) wallGrid[i][j] = true;
    if (i === i1 && j === j1) break;
    const e2 = 2 * err;
    if (e2 > -dj) { err -= dj; i += si; }
    if (e2 < di) { err += di; j += sj; }
  }
}

function rasterizeShape(shape: Shape, stepX: number, stepY: number, xMin: number, yMin: number, resolution: number, wallGrid: boolean[][]): void {
  const pts = shape.points;
  const maxSegLen = Math.min(stepX, stepY) * 0.3;
  const segments: [Point, Point][] = [];
  switch (shape.type) {
    case 'point': break;
    case 'line': if (pts.length >= 2) segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]); break;
    case 'rectangle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
        const left = Math.min(p1.x, p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x, p2.x);
        const bottom = Math.min(p1.y, p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y, p2.y);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: bottom }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: bottom }, { x: right, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: right, y: top }, { x: left, y: top }]);
        segments.push([{ x: left, y: top }, { x: left, y: bottom }]);
      } break;
    case 'circle': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        const center = pts[0];
        const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
        const steps = Math.max(20, Math.ceil(2 * Math.PI * radius / maxSegLen));
        let prev = { x: center.x + radius, y: center.y };
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const angle = (i / steps) * Math.PI * 2;
          const cur = { x: center.x + radius * Math.cos(angle), y: center.y + radius * Math.sin(angle) };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'triangle': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        segments.push([pts[0], pts[1]]);
        segments.push([pts[1], pts[2]]);
        segments.push([pts[2], pts[0]]);
      } break;
    case 'quadratic': if (pts.length >= 3) {
        const p0 = pts[0], p1 = pts[1], ctrl = pts[2];
        const approxLen = Math.hypot(p0.x - p1.x, p0.y - p1.y);
        const steps = Math.max(10, Math.ceil(approxLen / maxSegLen));
        let prev = p0;
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const t = i / steps;
          const mt = 1 - t;
          const x = mt * mt * p0.x + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.x + t * t * p1.x;
          const y = mt * mt * p0.y + 2 * mt * t * ctrl.y + t * t * p1.y;
          const cur = { x, y };
          segments.push([prev, cur]);
          prev = cur;
        }
      } break;
    case 'brush': if (pts.length >= 2) {
        for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) segments.push([pts[i], pts[i + 1]]);
      } break;
  }
  for (const [a, b] of segments) {
    rasterizeLine(a.x, a.y, b.x, b.y, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);
  }
}

// ========== 2. 连通区域识别（BFS）==========
export interface GridRegion {
  id: number;
  cells: { i: number; j: number }[];
  bounds: { minI: number; maxI: number; minJ: number; maxJ: number };
  seed: Point;
  touchesEdge: boolean;
}

export interface GridData {
  regionIdGrid: number[][];
  regions: GridRegion[];
  stepX: number; stepY: number;
  xMin: number; yMin: number;
  resolution: number;
}

export function computeGridRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500
): GridData {
  const { xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax } = worldBounds;
  const stepX = (xMax - xMin) / resolution;
  const stepY = (yMax - yMin) / resolution;

  const wallGrid: boolean[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(false));
  for (const shape of shapes) rasterizeShape(shape, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution, wallGrid);

  const regionIdGrid: number[][] = Array(resolution).fill(null).map(() => Array(resolution).fill(-1));
  const regions: GridRegion[] = [];
  let currentId = 0;
  const dirs = [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]];

  for (let i = 0; i < resolution; i++) {
    for (let j = 0; j < resolution; j++) {
      if (wallGrid[i][j] || regionIdGrid[i][j] !== -1) continue;
      const queue: [number, number][] = [[i, j]];
      regionIdGrid[i][j] = currentId;
      const cells: { i: number; j: number }[] = [{ i, j }];
      let minI = i, maxI = i, minJ = j, maxJ = j;
      let touchesEdge = (i === 0 || i === resolution-1 || j === 0 || j === resolution-1);
      while (queue.length) {
        const [ci, cj] = queue.shift()!;
        for (const [di, dj] of dirs) {
          const ni = ci + di, nj = cj + dj;
          if (ni >= 0 && ni < resolution && nj >= 0 && nj < resolution &&
              !wallGrid[ni][nj] && regionIdGrid[ni][nj] === -1) {
            regionIdGrid[ni][nj] = currentId;
            queue.push([ni, nj]);
            cells.push({ i: ni, j: nj });
            minI = Math.min(minI, ni); maxI = Math.max(maxI, ni);
            minJ = Math.min(minJ, nj); maxJ = Math.max(maxJ, nj);
            if (ni === 0 || ni === resolution-1 || nj === 0 || nj === resolution-1) touchesEdge = true;
          }
        }
      }
      let sumX = 0, sumY = 0;
      for (const cell of cells) {
        sumX += xMin + (cell.j + 0.5) * stepX;
        sumY += yMin + (cell.i + 0.5) * stepY;
      }
      const seed = { x: sumX / cells.length, y: sumY / cells.length };
      regions.push({ id: currentId, cells, bounds: { minI, maxI, minJ, maxJ }, seed, touchesEdge });
      currentId++;
    }
  }
  return { regionIdGrid, regions, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution };
}

// ========== 3. 射线与图形精确求交 ==========
function intersectLineSegment(origin: Point, dir: Point, a: Point, b: Point): number | null {
  const ax = a.x, ay = a.y, bx = b.x, by = b.y;
  const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
  const det = dir.x * dy - dir.y * dx;
  if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-12) return null;
  const t = ((ax - origin.x) * dy - (ay - origin.y) * dx) / det;
  if (t <= 1e-12) return null;
  const u = ((ax - origin.x) * dir.y - (ay - origin.y) * dir.x) / det;
  if (u >= -1e-12 && u <= 1 + 1e-12) return t;
  return null;
}

function intersectCircle(origin: Point, dir: Point, center: Point, radius: number): number | null {
  const ox = origin.x - center.x, oy = origin.y - center.y;
  const dx = dir.x, dy = dir.y;
  const a = dx*dx + dy*dy;
  const b = 2*(ox*dx + oy*dy);
  const c = ox*ox + oy*oy - radius*radius;
  const delta = b*b - 4*a*c;
  if (delta < 0) return null;
  const sqrtDelta = Math.sqrt(delta);
  const t1 = (-b - sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  const t2 = (-b + sqrtDelta)/(2*a);
  let tmin = null;
  if (t1 > 1e-12) tmin = t1;
  if (t2 > 1e-12 && (tmin === null || t2 < tmin)) tmin = t2;
  return tmin;
}

function intersectQuadratic(origin: Point, dir: Point, p0: Point, p1: Point, ctrl: Point, segments = 30): number | null {
  let minT: number | null = null;
  let prev = p0;
  for (let i = 1; i <= segments; i++) {
    const t = i / segments;
    const mt = 1 - t;
    const x = mt*mt*p0.x + 2*mt*t*ctrl.x + t*t*p1.x;
    const y = mt*mt*p0.y + 2*mt*t*ctrl.y + t*t*p1.y;
    const cur = { x, y };
    const tHit = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, prev, cur);
    if (tHit !== null && (minT === null || tHit < minT)) minT = tHit;
    prev = cur;
  }
  return minT;
}

function getNearestIntersection(origin: Point, dir: Point, shapes: Shape[]): { point: Point; t: number; shapeId?: string; segment?: [Point, Point] } | null {
  let bestT: number | null = null;
  let bestPoint: Point | null = null;
  let bestShapeId: string | undefined = undefined;
  let bestSegment: [Point, Point] | undefined = undefined;
  for (const shape of shapes) {
    const pts = shape.points;
    switch (shape.type) {
      case 'point': break;
      case 'line':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [pts[0], pts[1]];
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'rectangle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const p1 = pts[0], p2 = pts[1];
          const left = Math.min(p1.x,p2.x), right = Math.max(p1.x,p2.x);
          const bottom = Math.min(p1.y,p2.y), top = Math.max(p1.y,p2.y);
          const segs: [Point,Point][] = [
            [{x:left,y:bottom},{x:right,y:bottom}], [{x:right,y:bottom},{x:right,y:top}],
            [{x:right,y:top},{x:left,y:top}], [{x:left,y:top},{x:left,y:bottom}]
          ];
          for (const seg of segs) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, seg[0], seg[1]);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = seg;
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'circle':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          const center = pts[0];
          const radius = Math.hypot(pts[1].x - center.x, pts[1].y - center.y);
          const t = intersectCircle(origin, dir, center, radius);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id;
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'triangle':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          for (let i=0;i<3;i++) {
            const a = pts[i], b = pts[(i+1)%3];
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, a, b);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [a, b];
            }
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'quadratic':
        if (pts.length >= 3) {
          const t = intersectQuadratic(origin, dir, pts[0], pts[1], pts[2]);
          if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
            bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
            bestShapeId = shape.id;
          }
        }
        break;
      case 'brush':
        if (pts.length >= 2) {
          for (let i=0;i<pts.length-1;i++) {
            const t = intersectLineSegment(origin, dir, pts[i], pts[i+1]);
            if (t !== null && (bestT === null || t < bestT)) {
              bestT = t; bestPoint = { x: origin.x + dir.x*t, y: origin.y + dir.y*t };
              bestShapeId = shape.id; bestSegment = [pts[i], pts[i+1]];
            }
          }
        }
        break;
    }
  }
  if (bestPoint) {
    return { point: bestPoint, t: bestT!, shapeId: bestShapeId, segment: bestSegment };
  }
  return null;
}

// ========== 辅助：世界坐标转网格索引 ==========
function worldToGrid(
  x: number,
  y: number,
  xMin: number,
  yMin: number,
  stepX: number,
  stepY: number,
  resolution: number
): { i: number; j: number } | null {
  const j = Math.floor((x - xMin) / stepX);
  const i = Math.floor((y - yMin) / stepY);
  if (i >= 0 && i < resolution && j >= 0 && j < resolution) {
    return { i, j };
  }
  return null;
}

// ========== 在交点周围扫描网格，确定外部区域 ID ==========
function getOutsideIdAroundPoint(
  point: Point,
  gridData: GridData,
  rid: number,
  radius: number = 2
): number | null {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;
  const gridPos = worldToGrid(point.x, point.y, xMin, yMin, stepX, stepY, resolution);
  if (!gridPos) return null;
  const { i: ci, j: cj } = gridPos;

  type Candidate = { id: number; distSq: number };
  const candidates: Candidate[] = [];

  for (let di = -radius; di <= radius; di++) {
    for (let dj = -radius; dj <= radius; dj++) {
      const ni = ci + di;
      const nj = cj + dj;
      if (ni < 0 || ni >= resolution || nj < 0 || nj >= resolution) continue;
      const nid = regionIdGrid[ni][nj];
      if (nid === rid || nid === -1) continue;

      const centerX = xMin + (nj + 0.5) * stepX;
      const centerY = yMin + (ni + 0.5) * stepY;
      const dx = centerX - point.x;
      const dy = centerY - point.y;
      const distSq = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      candidates.push({ id: nid, distSq });
    }
  }

  if (candidates.length === 0) {
    if (radius < 4) {
      return getOutsideIdAroundPoint(point, gridData, rid, radius + 1);
    }
    return null;
  }

  const freqMap = new Map<number, { count: number; minDistSq: number }>();
  for (const cand of candidates) {
    const existing = freqMap.get(cand.id);
    if (existing) {
      existing.count++;
      if (cand.distSq < existing.minDistSq) existing.minDistSq = cand.distSq;
    } else {
      freqMap.set(cand.id, { count: 1, minDistSq: cand.distSq });
    }
  }

  let bestId: number | null = null;
  let bestCount = 0;
  let bestDistSq = Infinity;
  for (const [id, { count, minDistSq }] of freqMap.entries()) {
    if (count > bestCount || (count === bestCount && minDistSq < bestDistSq)) {
      bestCount = count;
      bestDistSq = minDistSq;
      bestId = id;
    }
  }
  return bestId;
}

// ========== 核心：收集边界点 ==========
export interface BoundaryPoint {
  point: Point;
  insideId: number;
  outsideId: number;
}

function collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(
  regionId: number,
  gridData: GridData,
  shapes: Shape[]
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  const { regionIdGrid, stepX, stepY, xMin, yMin, resolution } = gridData;

  const region = gridData.regions.find(r => r.id === regionId);
  if (!region) return [];

  const pointMap = new Map<string, BoundaryPoint>();

  const directions = [
    { x: 1, y: 0 }, { x: -1, y: 0 }, { x: 0, y: 1 }, { x: 0, y: -1 },
    { x: 0.7071067811865475, y: 0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: 0.7071067811865475, y: -0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: -0.7071067811865475, y: 0.7071067811865475 },
    { x: -0.7071067811865475, y: -0.7071067811865475 }
  ];

  for (const cell of region.cells) {
    const { i, j } = cell;
    const centerX = xMin + (j + 0.5) * stepX;
    const centerY = yMin + (i + 0.5) * stepY;
    const origin = { x: centerX, y: centerY };

    for (const dir of directions) {
      const hit = getNearestIntersection(origin, dir, shapes);
      if (!hit) continue;

      const hitPoint = hit.point;
      const outsideId = getOutsideIdAroundPoint(hitPoint, gridData, regionId);
      if (outsideId === null) continue;

      const key = `${Math.round(hitPoint.x * 1e9)}_${Math.round(hitPoint.y * 1e9)}`;
      const existing = pointMap.get(key);
      if (!existing) {
        pointMap.set(key, {
          point: hitPoint,
          insideId: regionId,
          outsideId: outsideId
        });
      } else if (existing.outsideId !== outsideId) {
        pointMap.delete(key);
      }
    }
  }

  return Array.from(pointMap.values());
}

export function groupBoundaryPointsByOutsideId(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[]
): Map<number, Point[]> {
  const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();

  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    const { outsideId, point } = bp;
    if (!groups.has(outsideId)) {
      groups.set(outsideId, []);
    }
    groups.get(outsideId)!.push(point);
  }

  return groups;
}

// ========== 4. 多边形构建（极角排序）==========
function buildClosedRing(points: Point[]): Point[] {
  if (points.length < 3) return [];
  const uniqueMap = new Map<string, Point>();
  for (const p of points) {
    const key = `${Math.round(p.x * 1e9)}_${Math.round(p.y * 1e9)}`;
    if (!uniqueMap.has(key)) uniqueMap.set(key, p);
  }
  let unique = Array.from(uniqueMap.values());
  if (unique.length < 3) return [];

  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const p of unique) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
  cx /= unique.length; cy /= unique.length;

  unique.sort((a, b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - cy, a.x - cx);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - cy, b.x - cx);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  if (Math.hypot(unique[0].x - unique[unique.length - 1].x, unique[0].y - unique[unique.length - 1].y) > 1e-6) {
    unique.push(unique[0]);
  }
  return unique;
}

function buildClosedRingsByDistanceGrouping(points: Point[], distanceThresholdFactor: number = 0.3): { rings: Point[][], groups: Point[][] } {
  if (points.length < 3) return { rings: [], groups: [] };

  const uniqueMap = new Map<string, Point>();
  for (const p of points) {
    const key = `${p.x.toFixed(9)},${p.y.toFixed(9)}`;
    if (!uniqueMap.has(key)) uniqueMap.set(key, p);
  }
  const unique = Array.from(uniqueMap.values());
  if (unique.length < 3) return { rings: [], groups: [] };

  let cx = 0, cy = 0;
  for (const p of unique) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
  cx /= unique.length; cy /= unique.length;

  const pointsWithInfo = unique.map(p => ({
    point: p,
    angle: Math.atan2(p.y - cy, p.x - cx),
    dist: Math.hypot(p.x - cx, p.y - cy)
  }));

  pointsWithInfo.sort((a, b) => a.angle - b.angle);

  const avgDist = pointsWithInfo.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.dist, 0) / pointsWithInfo.length;
  const distThreshold = avgDist * distanceThresholdFactor;

  const groups: Point[][] = [];
  let currentGroup: Point[] = [pointsWithInfo[0].point];
  const n = pointsWithInfo.length;

  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const prevDist = pointsWithInfo[i - 1].dist;
    const currDist = pointsWithInfo[i].dist;
    const distDiff = Math.abs(currDist - prevDist);

    if (distDiff > distThreshold && currentGroup.length >= 3) {
      groups.push(currentGroup);
      currentGroup = [pointsWithInfo[i].point];
    } else {
      currentGroup.push(pointsWithInfo[i].point);
    }
  }
  if (currentGroup.length >= 3) groups.push(currentGroup);
  else if (currentGroup.length > 0 && groups.length > 0) {
    groups[groups.length - 1].push(...currentGroup);
  }

  const rings: Point[][] = [];
  for (const group of groups) {
    const ring = buildClosedRing(group);
    if (ring.length >= 3) rings.push(ring);
  }
  return { rings, groups };
}

function polygonArea(points: Point[]): number {
  let area = 0;
  const n = points.length;
  for (let i = 0, j = n - 1; i < n; j = i++) {
    area += points[j].x * points[i].y - points[j].y * points[i].x;
  }
  return area / 2;
}

// ========== 5. 主函数：计算所有封闭区域 ==========
export function computeRegionsExact(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 500
): Point[][][] {
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  const mainRegions = gridData.regions.filter(r => !r.touchesEdge && r.cells.length >= 10);

  const result: Point[][][] = [];

  for (const region of mainRegions) {
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    if (boundaryPoints.length < 3) continue;

    const groups = groupBoundaryPointsByOutsideId(boundaryPoints);

    const rings: { points: Point[]; outsideId: number; area: number }[] = [];
    for (const [outsideId, pts] of groups.entries()) {
      if (pts.length < 3) continue;
      const ring = buildClosedRing(pts);
      if (ring.length >= 3) {
        const area = polygonArea(ring);
        rings.push({ points: ring, outsideId, area });
      }
    }

    if (rings.length === 0) continue;

    const outerCandidates = rings.filter(r => r.outsideId === -1 && r.area > 0);
    let outerRing = outerCandidates.length > 0 ? outerCandidates[0] : null;

    if (!outerRing) {
      const positiveRings = rings.filter(r => r.area > 0);
      if (positiveRings.length > 0) {
        positiveRings.sort((a, b) => b.area - a.area);
        outerRing = positiveRings[0];
      }
    }
    if (!outerRing) continue;

    if (outerRing.area < 0) {
      outerRing.points.reverse();
    }

    const innerRings = rings.filter(r => r !== outerRing);

    const regionPolygon: Point[][] = [outerRing.points, ...innerRings.map(r => r.points)];
    result.push(regionPolygon);
  }

  return result;
}

// ========== 6. 调试辅助 ==========
export interface DebugRay { start: Point; end: Point; direction: string; outsideId: number; }
export interface OutsideIdEndpoint {
  outsideId: number;
  insideId: number;
  p1: { x: number; y: number; distToCentroid: number };
  p2: { x: number; y: number; distToCentroid: number } | null;
}

export interface DebugRegionData {
  id: number; cellCount: number; bounds: any; seed: Point;
  boundaryPolygon: Point[]; rays: DebugRay[];
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[];
  clusteredBoundaryPoints?: BoundaryPoint[];
  rings?: Point[][];
  centroid?: Point | null;
  uniqueBoundaryPoints?: { point: Point; insideId: number; outsideId: number }[];
  pointGroups?: Point[][];
  outsideIdEndpoints?: OutsideIdEndpoint[];
  originalOutsideIdEndpoints?: OutsideIdEndpoint[];
}

// ========== 双重阈值辅助函数（新算法） ==========
function radialDist(p: Point, centroid: Point): number {
  return Math.hypot(p.x - centroid.x, p.y - centroid.y);
}

function meetsThreshold(
  a: Point,
  b: Point,
  centroid: Point,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): boolean {
  const euclidean = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
  if (euclidean >= distThreshold) return false;
  const radialA = radialDist(a, centroid);
  const radialB = radialDist(b, centroid);
  return Math.abs(radialA - radialB) < radialThreshold;
}

// ========== 第一次分组：将无序点集构建为有序片段 ==========
interface Segment {
  id: number;
  points: Point[];
  start: Point;
  end: Point;
  closed: boolean;
}

function groupIntoSegments(
  points: Point[],
  centroid: Point | null,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Segment[] {
  if (points.length === 0) return [];

  if (!centroid) {
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of points) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    centroid = { x: cx / points.length, y: cy / points.length };
  }

  const sorted = [...points];
  sorted.sort((a, b) => {
    const angleA = Math.atan2(a.y - centroid!.y, a.x - centroid!.x);
    const angleB = Math.atan2(b.y - centroid!.y, b.x - centroid!.x);
    return angleA - angleB;
  });

  const segments: { points: Point[]; last: Point }[] = [];
  for (const p of sorted) {
    let bestIdx = -1;
    let bestDist = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < segments.length; i++) {
      const last = segments[i].last;
      if (meetsThreshold(last, p, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
        const d = Math.hypot(last.x - p.x, last.y - p.y);
        if (d < bestDist) {
          bestDist = d;
          bestIdx = i;
        }
      }
    }
    if (bestIdx !== -1) {
      segments[bestIdx].points.push(p);
      segments[bestIdx].last = p;
    } else {
      segments.push({ points: [p], last: p });
    }
  }

  let nextId = 0;
  const result: Segment[] = [];
  for (const seg of segments) {
    if (seg.points.length === 0) continue;
    const start = seg.points[0];
    const end = seg.points[seg.points.length - 1];
    let closed = false;
    if (seg.points.length >= 3 && meetsThreshold(start, end, centroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold)) {
      closed = true;
    }
    result.push({ id: nextId++, points: seg.points, start, end, closed });
  }
  return result;
}

// 新版边界点重聚类函数（使用双重阈值）
export function reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle(
  boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[],
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): BoundaryPoint[] {
  if (boundaryPoints.length === 0) return [];

  const byOriginalId = new Map<number, { point: Point; insideId: number }[]>();
  for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
    if (!byOriginalId.has(bp.outsideId)) {
      byOriginalId.set(bp.outsideId, []);
    }
    byOriginalId.get(bp.outsideId)!.push({ point: bp.point, insideId: bp.insideId });
  }

  const newBoundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[] = [];
  let nextSegmentId = 1;

  for (const [origId, ptsWithInfo] of byOriginalId) {
    const points = ptsWithInfo.map(item => item.point);
    let cx = 0, cy = 0;
    for (const p of points) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
    const groupCentroid = { x: cx / points.length, y: cy / points.length };

    const segments = groupIntoSegments(points, groupCentroid, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
    for (const seg of segments) {
      const newId = nextSegmentId++;
      for (const pt of seg.points) {
        const original = ptsWithInfo.find(item => Math.hypot(item.point.x - pt.x, item.point.y - pt.y) < 1e-6);
        const insideId = original ? original.insideId : boundaryPoints[0].insideId;
        newBoundaryPoints.push({
          point: pt,
          insideId: insideId,
          outsideId: newId,
        });
      }
    }
  }

  console.log(`[reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle] 生成片段数量: ${nextSegmentId - 1}`);
  return newBoundaryPoints;
}

// ========== 调试主函数 getDebugRegions（已集成新聚类）==========
export function getDebugRegions(
  shapes: Shape[],
  worldBounds: { xMin: number; xMax: number; yMin: number; yMax: number },
  resolution: number = 300,
  distanceThresholdFactor: number = 0.5
): DebugRegionData[] {
  const gridData = computeGridRegions(shapes, worldBounds, resolution);
  const debug: DebugRegionData[] = [];
  for (const region of gridData.regions) {
    if (region.touchesEdge) continue;
    const boundaryPoints = collectBoundaryPointsForMainRegion(region.id, gridData, shapes);
    
    const step = Math.min(gridData.stepX, gridData.stepY);
    const distThreshold = step * 1.8;
    const radialThreshold = step * 0.6;
    const reclusteredPoints = reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle(boundaryPoints, distThreshold, radialThreshold);
    
    console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} 原始边界点: ${boundaryPoints.length}, 重新聚类后: ${reclusteredPoints.length}`);

    const allBoundaryPoints = reclusteredPoints.map(bp => bp.point);
    
    const uniqueBoundaryPoints: { point: Point; insideId: number; outsideId: number }[] = reclusteredPoints.map(bp => ({
      point: bp.point,
      insideId: bp.insideId,
      outsideId: bp.outsideId
    }));
    
    const uniquePoints = uniqueBoundaryPoints.map(ub => ub.point);
    
    console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} 重新聚类后点数: ${uniqueBoundaryPoints.length}`);
    
    const { rings: ringsFromSegments, groups: pointGroups } = buildClosedRingsByDistanceGrouping(uniquePoints, distanceThresholdFactor);
    console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} 距离分组成环数量: ${ringsFromSegments.length}`);
    ringsFromSegments.forEach((ring, idx) => {
      console.log(`  环${idx}: ${ring.length} 个顶点`);
    });
    console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} 距离分组数量: ${pointGroups.length}`);
    pointGroups.forEach((group, idx) => {
      console.log(`  组${idx}: ${group.length} 个点`);
    });

    let boundaryPolygon: Point[] = [];
    if (boundaryPoints.length >= 3) {
      const groups = new Map<number, Point[]>();
      for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
        if (!groups.has(bp.outsideId)) groups.set(bp.outsideId, []);
        groups.get(bp.outsideId)!.push(bp.point);
      }
      const outerPts = groups.get(-1);
      if (outerPts && outerPts.length >= 3) boundaryPolygon = buildClosedRing(outerPts);
    }

    const originalOutsideIdEndpoints: OutsideIdEndpoint[] = [];
    if (boundaryPoints.length > 0) {
      const origCentroid = { x: 0, y: 0 };
      for (const bp of boundaryPoints) { origCentroid.x += bp.point.x; origCentroid.y += bp.point.y; }
      origCentroid.x /= boundaryPoints.length;
      origCentroid.y /= boundaryPoints.length;
      
      const byOriginalOutside = new Map<number, { point: Point; insideId: number }[]>();
      for (const bp of boundaryPoints) {
        if (!byOriginalOutside.has(bp.outsideId)) {
          byOriginalOutside.set(bp.outsideId, []);
        }
        byOriginalOutside.get(bp.outsideId)!.push({ point: bp.point, insideId: bp.insideId });
      }
      
      for (const [outsideId, points] of byOriginalOutside) {
        if (points.length < 1) continue;
        
        points.sort((a, b) => {
          const angleA = Math.atan2(a.point.y - origCentroid.y, a.point.x - origCentroid.x);
          const angleB = Math.atan2(b.point.y - origCentroid.y, b.point.x - origCentroid.x);
          return angleA - angleB;
        });
        
        const p1 = points[0];
        const dist1 = Math.hypot(p1.point.x - origCentroid.x, p1.point.y - origCentroid.y);
        
        let p2Data: { x: number; y: number; distToCentroid: number } | null = null;
        if (points.length >= 2) {
          const p2 = points[points.length - 1];
          const dist2 = Math.hypot(p2.point.x - origCentroid.x, p2.point.y - origCentroid.y);
          p2Data = { x: p2.point.x, y: p2.point.y, distToCentroid: dist2 };
        }
        
        originalOutsideIdEndpoints.push({
          outsideId,
          insideId: p1.insideId,
          p1: { x: p1.point.x, y: p1.point.y, distToCentroid: dist1 },
          p2: p2Data,
        });
      }
    }

    let centroid: Point | null = null;
    if (allBoundaryPoints.length > 0) {
      let cx = 0, cy = 0;
      for (const p of allBoundaryPoints) { cx += p.x; cy += p.y; }
      centroid = { x: cx / allBoundaryPoints.length, y: cy / allBoundaryPoints.length };
    }
    
    const outsideIdEndpoints: OutsideIdEndpoint[] = [];
    if (centroid) {
      const byOutsideId = new Map<number, { point: Point; insideId: number }[]>();
      for (const ub of uniqueBoundaryPoints) {
        if (!byOutsideId.has(ub.outsideId)) {
          byOutsideId.set(ub.outsideId, []);
        }
        byOutsideId.get(ub.outsideId)!.push({ point: ub.point, insideId: ub.insideId });
      }
      
      for (const [outsideId, points] of byOutsideId) {
        if (points.length < 1) continue;
        
        points.sort((a, b) => {
          const angleA = Math.atan2(a.point.y - centroid.y, a.point.x - centroid.x);
          const angleB = Math.atan2(b.point.y - centroid.y, b.point.x - centroid.x);
          return angleA - angleB;
        });
        
        const p1 = points[0];
        const dist1 = Math.hypot(p1.point.x - centroid.x, p1.point.y - centroid.y);
        
        let p2Data: { x: number; y: number; distToCentroid: number } | null = null;
        if (points.length >= 2) {
          const p2 = points[points.length - 1];
          const dist2 = Math.hypot(p2.point.x - centroid.x, p2.point.y - centroid.y);
          p2Data = { x: p2.point.x, y: p2.point.y, distToCentroid: dist2 };
        }
        
        outsideIdEndpoints.push({
          outsideId,
          insideId: p1.insideId,
          p1: { x: p1.point.x, y: p1.point.y, distToCentroid: dist1 },
          p2: p2Data,
        });
        
        if (p2Data) {
          console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} o:${outsideId} 端点: p1(${p1.point.x.toFixed(3)},${p1.point.y.toFixed(3)}) d1=${dist1.toFixed(3)}, p2(${p2Data.x.toFixed(3)},${p2Data.y.toFixed(3)}) d2=${p2Data.distToCentroid.toFixed(3)}`);
        } else {
          console.log(`[调试] 区域 ${region.id} o:${outsideId} 端点: p1(${p1.point.x.toFixed(3)},${p1.point.y.toFixed(3)}) d1=${dist1.toFixed(3)} (单点)`);
        }
      }
    }

    const rays: DebugRay[] = [];
    debug.push({
      id: region.id,
      cellCount: region.cells.length,
      bounds: region.bounds,
      seed: region.seed,
      boundaryPolygon,
      rays,
      boundaryPoints,
      clusteredBoundaryPoints: [],
      rings: ringsFromSegments,
      centroid,
      uniqueBoundaryPoints,
      pointGroups,
      outsideIdEndpoints,
      originalOutsideIdEndpoints,
    });
  }
  return debug;
}

// 保留旧函数兼容（避免外部调用报错）
export function sortAndClusterBoundaryPoints(boundaryPoints: BoundaryPoint[], threshold: number): BoundaryPoint[] {
  console.warn("sortAndClusterBoundaryPoints is deprecated, use reclusterBoundaryPointsByPolarAngle");
  return boundaryPoints;
}
export interface ScanlineSpan { y: number; xMin: number; xMax: number; }
export type ScanlineCache = Record<number, ScanlineSpan[]>;
export function computeScanlineIntervals(gridData: GridData): ScanlineCache { return {}; }

1. 新增函数定义（添加到 regionDetectionExact.ts）
typescript
// 定义 segment 结构（应包含 closed 标志）
export interface SegmentForMatching {
  points: Point[];
  start: Point;
  end: Point;
  closed: boolean;        // 第一次分组时是否已自身首尾满足闭合条件
}

// 开放环结构
interface OpenRing {
  points: Point[];
  first: Point;
  last: Point;
}

/**
 * 通过端点匹配将多个 segment 组合成闭合环
 * @param segments 片段列表（每个片段包含点集、起点、终点、是否自身闭合）
 * @param centroid 全局重心（用于计算径向距离）
 * @param distThreshold 欧氏距离阈值（世界单位）
 * @param radialThreshold 径向距差阈值
 * @returns 闭合环列表，每个环是一个点数组（首尾闭合）
 */
export function buildClosedRingsFromSegments(
  segments: SegmentForMatching[],
  centroid: Point,
  distThreshold: number,
  radialThreshold: number
): Point[][] {
  if (segments.length === 0) return [];

  // 辅助函数：计算两点是否满足双重阈值
  function meetsThreshold(a: Point, b: Point): boolean {
    const euclidean = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y);
    if (euclidean >= distThreshold) return false;
    const radialA = Math.hypot(a.x - centroid.x, a.y - centroid.y);
    const radialB = Math.hypot(b.x - centroid.x, b.y - centroid.y);
    return Math.abs(radialA - radialB) < radialThreshold;
  }

  // 记录每个 segment 是否已被使用
  const used = new Array(segments.length).fill(false);
  const openRings: OpenRing[] = [];
  const closedRings: Point[][] = [];

  // 辅助：尝试将 segment 以指定方向连接到环的某个端点
  function tryConnect(
    ring: OpenRing,
    seg: SegmentForMatching,
    connectToFirst: boolean,
    connectWithStart: boolean
  ): { success: boolean; newPoints: Point[] } {
    const ringPoint = connectToFirst ? ring.first : ring.last;
    const segPoint = connectWithStart ? seg.start : seg.end;

    if (!meetsThreshold(ringPoint, segPoint)) return { success: false, newPoints: [] };

    // 构建合并后的点集
    let merged: Point[];
    if (connectToFirst) {
      // 连接到环头部
      const segmentPoints = connectWithStart ? seg.points : [...seg.points].reverse();
      merged = [...segmentPoints, ...ring.points];
    } else {
      // 连接到环尾部
      const segmentPoints = connectWithStart ? seg.points.slice(1) : [...seg.points].reverse().slice(1);
      merged = [...ring.points, ...segmentPoints];
    }
    return { success: true, newPoints: merged };
  }

  // 遍历所有 segment，尝试匹配开放环
  for (let idx = 0; idx < segments.length; idx++) {
    if (used[idx]) continue;
    const seg = segments[idx];
    let matched = false;

    // 尝试匹配现有开放环
    for (let i = 0; i < openRings.length; i++) {
      const ring = openRings[i];
      // 四种连接方式：环头+seg起点、环头+seg终点、环尾+seg起点、环尾+seg终点
      const ways = [
        { connectToFirst: true,  connectWithStart: true },  // 头+起点
        { connectToFirst: true,  connectWithStart: false }, // 头+终点
        { connectToFirst: false, connectWithStart: true },  // 尾+起点
        { connectToFirst: false, connectWithStart: false }  // 尾+终点
      ];
      for (const way of ways) {
        const result = tryConnect(ring, seg, way.connectToFirst, way.connectWithStart);
        if (result.success) {
          // 更新开放环
          openRings[i] = {
            points: result.newPoints,
            first: result.newPoints[0],
            last: result.newPoints[result.newPoints.length - 1]
          };
          used[idx] = true;
          matched = true;

          // 检查当前环首尾是否闭合
          const firstPt = openRings[i].first;
          const lastPt = openRings[i].last;
          if (meetsThreshold(firstPt, lastPt)) {
            // 闭合：移入结果
            const closedRing = openRings.splice(i, 1)[0].points;
            // 确保首尾闭合（添加起点到末尾，如果还不闭合的话）
            if (Math.hypot(closedRing[0].x - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].x,
                           closedRing[0].y - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].y) > 1e-6) {
              closedRing.push(closedRing[0]);
            }
            closedRings.push(closedRing);
          }
          break;
        }
      }
      if (matched) break;
    }

    // 未匹配任何开放环，则创建新开放环
    if (!matched) {
      openRings.push({
        points: [...seg.points],
        first: seg.start,
        last: seg.end
      });
      used[idx] = true;
    }
  }

  // 处理剩余的开放环：如果某个环自身已闭合（即首尾满足阈值），则作为闭合环加入
  for (const ring of openRings) {
    if (meetsThreshold(ring.first, ring.last) && ring.points.length >= 3) {
      let closedRing = [...ring.points];
      if (Math.hypot(closedRing[0].x - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].x,
                     closedRing[0].y - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].y) > 1e-6) {
        closedRing.push(closedRing[0]);
      }
      closedRings.push(closedRing);
    } else {
      // 此外，对于从未匹配过的 segment 且自身 closed 标志为 true 的情况，单独处理
      // （但上述 openRings 中可能包含这种 segment，已经检查过首尾闭合）
    }
  }

  // 额外处理孤立的自闭 segment（尽管它们可能已被 openRings 包含，但若未被闭合条件捕获，再显式处理一次）
  for (let idx = 0; idx < segments.length; idx++) {
    if (!used[idx] && segments[idx].closed && segments[idx].points.length >= 3) {
      const seg = segments[idx];
      if (meetsThreshold(seg.start, seg.end)) {
        let closedRing = [...seg.points];
        if (Math.hypot(closedRing[0].x - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].x,
                       closedRing[0].y - closedRing[closedRing.length-1].y) > 1e-6) {
          closedRing.push(closedRing[0]);
        }
        closedRings.push(closedRing);
      }
    }
  }

  return closedRings;
}
2. 修改 getDebugRegions 函数，使用新组环逻辑
在 regionDetectionExact.ts 中找到 getDebugRegions 函数，定位以下代码段：

typescript
const { rings: ringsFromSegments, groups: pointGroups } = buildClosedRingsByDistanceGrouping(uniquePoints, distanceThresholdFactor);

// 构建 segments 列表（基于 reclusteredPoints 的分组）
const segmentsMap = new Map<number, { points: Point[]; start: Point; end: Point; closed: boolean }>();
for (const bp of reclusteredPoints) {
  if (!segmentsMap.has(bp.outsideId)) {
    segmentsMap.set(bp.outsideId, { points: [], start: bp.point, end: bp.point, closed: false });
  }
  const seg = segmentsMap.get(bp.outsideId)!;
  seg.points.push(bp.point);
  // 更新 end（最后一个加入的点）

  // 注意：起点保持不变（第一个加入的点）

// 计算每个 segment 是否自身闭合（首尾距离 < distThreshold 且径向距差 < radialThreshold）
const globalCentroid = { x: 0, y: 0 };
let totalPoints = 0;
for (const seg of segmentsMap.values()) {
  for (const p of seg.points) {
    globalCentroid.x += p.x;
    globalCentroid.y += p.y;
    totalPoints++;
  }
}

  globalCentroid.x /= totalPoints;
  globalCentroid.y /= totalPoints;
}

  const start = seg.points[0];
  const end = seg.points[seg.points.length - 1];
  const euclidean = Math.hypot(start.x - end.x, start.y - end.y);
  const radialStart = Math.hypot(start.x - globalCentroid.x, start.y - globalCentroid.y);
  const radialEnd = Math.hypot(end.x - globalCentroid.x, end.y - globalCentroid.y);
  const closed = (euclidean < distThreshold && Math.abs(radialStart - radialEnd) < radialThreshold);
  segmentsList.push({
    points: seg.points,
    start,
    end,
    closed
  });
}

  segmentsList,
  globalCentroid,
  distThreshold,
  radialThreshold
);
